If the project only edited `site/`, there are no conflicts in the core. The skeleton's sample files in `site/` (sample controllers and templates) may also have changed; in that case keep the project's version.

## Versions

| Version         | Meaning |
| :-------------- | :------ |
| Patch (5.0.x)   | Fixes inside the core. `core:update` alone is enough |
| Minor (5.x.0)   | New features. Optional work for projects is described in `UPGRADE.md` |
| Major (x.0.0)   | Changes that require fixes to project code |

The full text, including the release procedure for skeleton maintainers, is in [docs/updating.md](https://github.com/lionheart-group/lh-fegg-skelton/blob/master/docs/updating.md) of the skeleton repository.


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/first/config/environments ---


# Environments and secrets

Per-server values and secrets (the environment, the database, API keys, passwords) are never committed, not even in `settings.php`. Read them with `feggEnv()`:

```php
$secret = feggEnv('FEGG_RECAPTCHA_SECRET', '');   // feggEnv(string $name, mixed $default = null)
```

`feggEnv()` returns the first of:

1. the environment variable with that name (`SetEnv` in Apache, `fastcgi_param` in nginx, Docker `environment` ...),
2. the key in **`htdocs/_fegg/site/config/env.php`**,
3. the key in the committed environment file **`htdocs/_fegg/site/config/env/<name>.php`** chosen for this server by its path (see [Test servers deployed with git pull](#test-servers-deployed-with-git-pull)),
4. `$default`.

`SetEnv` reaches only the web. `fegg-cli` (and a `fegg-cli migration` run after a deploy) sees only its own shell's environment and the files, so keep database settings in a file when the server runs `fegg-cli`.

`env.php` returns a PHP array. It is in `.gitignore` and is created on each server by copying `site/config/env.php.dist`:

```php title="htdocs/_fegg/site/config/env.php"
<?php
defined('FEGG_CORE_DIR') || exit;

return array(
    // production | staging | development
    'FEGG_ENV' => 'development',

    // Database. Default: SQLite file in site/data/db/db.db
    // 'FEGG_DB_DSN'      => 'mysql:host=127.0.0.1;port=3306;dbname=example;charset=utf8mb4',
    // 'FEGG_DB_USER'     => '',
    // 'FEGG_DB_PASSWORD' => '',

    // Contact form spam protection
    'FEGG_RECAPTCHA_SITE_KEY' => '',
    'FEGG_RECAPTCHA_SECRET'   => '',
    'FEGG_TURNSTILE_SITE_KEY' => '',
    'FEGG_TURNSTILE_SECRET'   => '',
);
```

| Name | Used for |
| :--- | :------- |
| `FEGG_ENV` | `production` (default; also for unknown values) / `staging` / `development`. Available as the constant `FEGG_ENV` |
| `FEGG_DB_DSN`, `FEGG_DB_USER`, `FEGG_DB_PASSWORD`, `FEGG_DB_SLAVE_DSN` | Database connection (see [Database](./index.mdx#database)) |
| `FEGG_DB_PATH` | SQLite file path, when `FEGG_DB_DSN` is not set |
| `FEGG_RECAPTCHA_SITE_KEY`, `FEGG_RECAPTCHA_SECRET`, `FEGG_TURNSTILE_SITE_KEY`, `FEGG_TURNSTILE_SECRET` | Contact form spam protection (see [Captcha](../../library/captcha/index.mdx)) |
| `FEGG_SMTP_PASSWORD` (`FEGG_SMTP_HOST`, `FEGG_SMTP_USERNAME`) | SMTP sending (see [Mail](../../library/mail/index.mdx#smtp)) |
| `FEGG_LOG_DIR` | Directory of the application log instead of `site/data/log` |
| `FEGG_UPLOAD_DIR` | Another directory for CMS uploads (default `htdocs/_fegg/site/up_dir`, see `site/config/upload_dir.php`). The web server must serve it at `/_fegg/site/up_dir/` |
| `FEGG_RECAPTCHA_SCORE_THRESHOLD` | reCAPTCHA score needed (default `0.5`; `1.0` rejects every form, to check the error message) |
| `FEGG_CMS_INITIAL_LOGIN`, `FEGG_CMS_INITIAL_NAME`, `FEGG_CMS_INITIAL_PASSWORD_HASH` (or `FEGG_CMS_INITIAL_PASSWORD`) | First CMS user, created by [`fegg-cli migration`](../cli.mdx#migration) |
| `FEGG_ENV_NAME` | Environment variable only: chooses `site/config/env/<name>.php` instead of `paths.php` |

Add your own keys the same way and read them with `feggEnv('NAME', $default)`.

:::warning

**A site without `env.php` and without a matching environment file runs as production.** On your machine, create `env.php` with `'FEGG_ENV' => 'development'`. Otherwise developers do not see error details there. Test servers on the LionHeart develop server run as `staging` without any file of their own (next section).

:::

`FEGG_ENV` decides:

- whether [developer mode](#developer-mode) is possible (not in production),
- whether the contact form sends the administrator mail to the test address (outside production),
- the default `log_level` (`warning` in production, `debug` elsewhere).

## Test servers deployed with git pull

On the LionHeart develop server (`http://p83.<project>.develop.lionheart.co.jp/`, project directory `/var/www/vhosts/<project>/p83`) a deploy is `git pull` followed by `fegg-cli migration`. Nothing can be placed on the server by hand, so the test server's settings are committed:

```php title="htdocs/_fegg/site/config/env/paths.php"
return array(
    // project root pattern (fnmatch; * and ? do not cross /) => environment file name
    '/var/www/vhosts/*/p[0-9]*' => 'staging',
);
```

```php title="htdocs/_fegg/site/config/env/staging.php"
return array(
    'FEGG_ENV' => 'staging',
    // first CMS user, created by fegg-cli migration (see below)
    'FEGG_CMS_INITIAL_LOGIN'         => 'admin',
    'FEGG_CMS_INITIAL_PASSWORD_HASH' => '$2y$10$...',   // ./fegg-cli user:hash
    // Turnstile test keys (no host name registration needed): always pass (2x00000000000000000000AB / 2x0000000000000000000000000000000AA always fail)
    'FEGG_TURNSTILE_SITE_KEY' => '1x00000000000000000000AA',
    'FEGG_TURNSTILE_SECRET'   => '1x0000000000000000000000000000000AA',
);
```

Both files come with the skeleton. The project root (the directory of `fegg-cli`) is matched against `paths.php`; the first match chooses `site/config/env/<name>.php`. Other paths (production, your machine) are unaffected.

- **Why the path:** the host name of a request is chosen by the client. Matching it would let a request to production with `Host: x.develop.lionheart.co.jp` switch production to the test settings (test captcha keys that always pass, the test administrator). The path is set by the server, and `fegg-cli` sees the same path as the web.
- **Check it:** the first line of `fegg-cli migration` in the deploy log names the environment and its file (see [fegg-cli](../cli.mdx#migration)).
- **First CMS user:** `fegg-cli migration` creates it once, while `cms_user` is empty (see [fegg-cli](../cli.mdx#migration)). Commit the hash (`./fegg-cli user:hash`), not the password, and change the password after the first login.

:::warning

Everyone who can read the repository can read these files. Put only values made for the test server there, never production keys or passwords. `env.php` on a server and environment variables still win over them.

:::

## Developer mode

Fegg runs in developer mode (the constant `FEGG_DEVELOPER` is `'1'`) only when **both** are true:

- the request comes from an address in `developer_ip`, and
- `FEGG_ENV` is not `production`.

In developer mode:

- PHP errors are displayed.
- An uncaught exception shows the message, the file and line, the template line (`Template: <file>.tpl:<line>`), the stack trace and `$this->page`. Other visitors get the error page (see [Logging and errors](../../basic/logging.mdx)). In both cases the error is written to the PHP error log and the application log.
- The 404 page shows which controller file Fegg tried to load.

`developer_ip` alone is not a security boundary: behind a proxy or load balancer every request may come from the same address.


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/first/config ---


import { SiblingDocCardList } from '@site/src/components/CategoryDocCardList';

# Settings

> The project's settings in `site/config/`. Per-server values and secrets (`env.php`, the test servers' settings, developer mode) are on their own page:

<SiblingDocCardList ids={["environments"]} />

## Basic settings

The defaults are in `htdocs/_fegg/core/config/settings.php`. Do not edit that file (it is replaced on update). Put the values for your project in **`htdocs/_fegg/site/config/settings.php`**.

The core file is loaded first, then the site file. Only the keys the site file sets are changed, so write one key per line and never reassign the whole array (`$settings = array(...)` would drop the defaults).

```php title="htdocs/_fegg/site/config/settings.php"
<?php
defined('FEGG_CORE_DIR') || exit;

// Run mode  1: running, empty: under maintenance
$settings['run_mode'] = '1';

// Maintenance page URL
$settings['maintenance_page_url'] = '';

// Developer IP addresses (multiple allowed). Error details only outside production (FEGG_ENV)
$settings['developer_ip'] = array('192.168.65.1', '203.0.113.10');

// Basic authentication scope (0: none, 1: whole site, 2: partial)
#$settings['basic_auth_scope'] = 0;
#$settings['basic_auth_user'] = '';
#$settings['basic_auth_password'] = '';

// HSTS, once the whole site is served over HTTPS
#$settings['hsts'] = 'max-age=31536000';
```

| Key                    | Default                         | Remarks |
| :--------------------- | :------------------------------ | :------ |
| `cms`                  | `true`                          | `false` for a site without the CMS (see [A site without the CMS](#a-site-without-the-cms)) |
| `run_mode`             | `'1'`                           | `'1'`: running. Empty: under maintenance. During maintenance every request that does not come from `developer_ip` is redirected to `maintenance_page_url`, or gets a short maintenance message when it is empty. |
| `maintenance_page_url` | `''`                            | Redirect target during maintenance |
| `developer_ip`         | `array()`                       | Addresses that can see error details outside production (see [Developer mode](./environments.mdx#developer-mode)) and that bypass maintenance |
| `timezone`             | `''`                            | Time zone of the application, set once at startup. `''`: `FEGG_DEFAULT_TIMEZONE` (`Asia/Tokyo`) |
| `log_level`            | `''`                            | Lowest level written to the application log (`debug`, `info`, `notice`, `warning`, `error` ...). `''`: `warning` in production, `debug` elsewhere. See [Log](../../library/log/index.mdx#settings) |
| `log_retention_days`   | `30`                            | Days to keep the application log files (`0`: keep all) |
| `mail`                 | `array('transport' => 'sendmail', 'from' => '', 'from_name' => '')` | Outgoing mail of `Fegg\Tool\Mail`, and the sender when `send()` is given none (see [Sender](../../library/mail/index.mdx#sender)). For SMTP see [Mail](../../library/mail/index.mdx#smtp) |
| `security_headers`     | `array()`                       | Changes the default security headers: `'Header-Name' => 'value'`, `''` removes one. See [Security](../../basic/security.mdx#security-headers) |
| `hsts`                 | `''`                            | `Strict-Transport-Security` value sent on HTTPS responses, e.g. `'max-age=31536000'`. `''`: not sent |
| `csp`                  | `''`                            | `Content-Security-Policy` value. `''`: not sent |
| `csp_report_only`      | `false`                         | `true`: send the policy as `Content-Security-Policy-Report-Only` (reports only, blocks nothing) |
| `default_language`     | not set                         | Language code used when none is detected, e.g. `'ja'`. Only for multilingual sites |
| `support_language`     | not set                         | Supported language codes, e.g. `array('ja', 'en')`. Only for multilingual sites |
| `basic_auth_scope`     | `0`                             | `0`: no Basic authentication. `1`: every request handled by Fegg, checked before the controller runs. `2`: only where the controller calls `$this->basicAuth()` |
| `basic_auth_user`      | `''`                            | Basic authentication user |
| `basic_auth_password`  | `''`                            | Basic authentication password (compared with `hash_equals()`) |
| `basic_auth_message`   | not set                         | Realm shown in the browser's login dialog |
| `basic_auth_error_message` | not set                     | Text sent with the 401 response when the login fails (default `Authorization Required`) |
| `template_dir`         | `site/template`, `core/template` | Template directories, searched in this order. The `tpl/` of the site the URL belongs to is put first (see [Sites](../../basic/templates/index.mdx#sites)) |
| `template_ext`         | `'tpl'`                         | Template file extension |
| `template_cache_dir`   | `site/data/cache/template`      | Compiled template cache |
| `global_config_dir`    | `''`                            | Optional directory searched by `loadConfig()` before `site/config/`. End it with `/` |

Read a setting from a controller with [`$this->getSetting()`](../../basic/others/config/getsetting.mdx).

### A site without the CMS

```php title="htdocs/_fegg/site/config/settings.php"
$settings['cms'] = false;
```

With the CMS turned off (`Fegg\Cms\Setting`):

- `/cms/...` (the core's and the project's CMS screens) and the uploads' controller (image sizes and `/file/<id>/` under `/_fegg/site/up_dir/`) answer 404,
- [`fegg-cli migration`](../cli.mdx#migration) neither creates nor drops `cms_user`, `cms_file` and `cms_image` (their rows are kept) and creates no first CMS user; `fegg-cli db:schema` leaves them out,
- `fegg-cli user:*` refuses to run.

Remove what the project itself has for the CMS: `site/config/database/cms_news.yaml` and the other post types (migration then drops those tables), `site/src/Controller/Cms/`, `site/template/cms/`, the news pages if they show CMS posts, and `site/config/cms_menu.php`.

## Database

The connection is set in **`htdocs/_fegg/site/config/database_config.php`**, which reads `FEGG_DB_DSN`, `FEGG_DB_USER`, `FEGG_DB_PASSWORD`, `FEGG_DB_SLAVE_DSN` and `FEGG_DB_PATH` with `feggEnv()`, so the file is the same on every server. Without settings it is the SQLite file `htdocs/_fegg/site/data/db/db.db`; if the server allows it, move it **outside the document root** with `FEGG_DB_PATH`. For MySQL / MariaDB and master / slave, see [Database configuration](../../library/database/configuration.mdx).

Tables are defined in YAML files (`core/config/database/` for the CMS tables, `site/config/database/` for the project's tables) and created or changed with `./fegg-cli migration` (see [fegg-cli](../cli.mdx#migration)). Every table needs the columns `valid`, `created` and `modified`. The files are read by [Fegg\Tool\Yaml](../../library/yaml/index.mdx), which says what it reads.

## Other files in `site/config/`

| File                  | Remarks |
| :-------------------- | :------ |
| `route.php`           | URL routing (see [URL](../../basic/url.mdx#routing)) |
| `sites.php`           | The project's sites, one line each (see [Sites](../../basic/templates/index.mdx#sites)) |
| `site_info.php`       | Site title, description, OGP image, `picture` breakpoints, ... Loaded by the base controller and available in templates as `$site`. A site's `'info'` in `sites.php` overrides it for that site |
| `category_info.php`   | Labels for categories and the CMS visibility states. Loaded by the base controller into `$this->config['category_info']` |
| `cms_menu.php`        | CMS side menu. Add one block per post type |
| `env.php`             | Per-server settings and secrets, read with `feggEnv()`. Not committed; copy `env.php.dist` (see [Environments and secrets](./environments.mdx)) |
| `database_config.php` | Database connection, from `feggEnv()` (see [Database](#database)) |
| `upload_dir.php`      | Path and URL of the CMS upload directory (`htdocs/_fegg/site/up_dir`, `/_fegg/site/up_dir`), and the limits of images in other sizes (`image_max`, `image_cache_max`, see [Trim](../../library/trim/index.mdx#images-of-the-cms-in-other-sizes)) |
| `database/*.yaml`     | Table definitions for `fegg-cli migration` |
| `define.php`          | Optional. Project constants, loaded right after the core starts (web and CLI) |
| `fegg_update.php`     | Optional. Repository used by `fegg-cli core:update` (see [Updating the core](../update.mdx)) |

Any other file in `site/config/` can be loaded with [`$this->loadConfig()`](../../basic/others/config/loadconfig.mdx). A file with the same name as one in `core/config/` (for example `validation_error_message.php`) replaces the core's file.


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/first/migration ---


import { SiblingDocCardList } from '@site/src/components/CategoryDocCardList';

# Moving a Fegg 3 / 4 project

Fegg 5 keeps no compatibility with Fegg 3 / 4 code: there is no `getClass()`, no Fegg 4 class names (`MyApplication`, `Model`, `DB_pdo`, `LH_FW` ...), and controllers without a namespace are not dispatched. A project is not upgraded in place: **start a new project from the Fegg 5 skeleton and bring over what belongs to the project**, rewriting its PHP in the Fegg 5 style. The database, the uploaded files, the templates and the front-end files carry over. Once rebuilt, the project is updated with `fegg-cli core:update` like any Fegg 5 project.

This page is a summary. The full steps, with the commands and the complete tables, are in [docs/migration-v3-v4-to-v5.md](https://github.com/lionheart-group/lh-fegg-skelton/blob/master/docs/migration-v3-v4-to-v5.md) of the skeleton repository.

Work on a new branch of the project repository (the old code stays on the old branch) and test against a copy of the production database.

## 1. List what the old project has of its own

Find the skeleton version the project started from (`version` in `package.json`, or `@version` in `fegg/Application.php`). Check out the skeleton at that tag, copy the project's core files over it (`fegg/`, `code/lib/`, `code/application/cms/`, `code/template/cms/`, `htdocs/cms/`, `htdocs/index.php`, `htdocs/.htaccess`) and look at `git diff`. Write down:

- **Changes to the core**: Fegg 5 rewrote most of the core, so decide for each whether the project still needs it. What is still needed is written again as an override in `site/` (see [Controller](../../basic/cms/controllers.mdx#extending-a-core-controller)).
- **The project's own code**: controllers (public pages and post types), project libraries in `code/lib/`, `code/lib/Modifire.php`, processing added to `MyApplication`.
- **The project's own files**: templates, `htdocs/data/` (SCSS, JS, images), table definitions, config values, `code/data/`, uploads.

## 2. Start from the Fegg 5 skeleton

1. Replace the branch's contents with the Fegg 5 skeleton (everything except `.git`) and rename it as for any new project (the package name in `package.json`, the local URL in `e2e/playwright.config.ts`).
2. Copy `site/config/env.php.dist` to `site/config/env.php` on each development or staging server and set `'FEGG_ENV' => 'development'`: without it the site runs as production and shows no error details (see [Settings](../config/environments.mdx)).
3. Set the values the project changed in the old `fegg/settings.php` (`developer_ip`, `run_mode`, `maintenance_page_url`, Basic authentication, languages) in `site/config/settings.php`.
4. Add the project's own rules of the old `htdocs/.htaccess` (redirects, headers) to the new one. If the project changed the paths in `index.php`, set the three constants (`FEGG_HTML_DIR`, `FEGG_CORE_DIR`, `FEGG_SITE_DIR`) to match (see [Directory structure](../cont.mdx#entry-files-and-layout)).

## 3. Bring over the files that carry over

| v3 / v4                                                          | Fegg 5                                                     |
| :--------------------------------------------------------------- | :--------------------------------------------------------- |
| `htdocs/data/` (SCSS, JS, images)                                | `htdocs/_fegg/site/template/main/` (`scss/`, `js/`, `img/`; `npm run build:scss`) |
| `htdocs/cms/scss/unique.scss` (the project's CMS styles)         | `htdocs/_fegg/site/template/cms/scss/unique.scss`          |
| `code/template/` except `cms/`                                  | `htdocs/_fegg/site/template/main/tpl/` (check with `./fegg-cli template:lint`) |
| `code/template/cms/<post type>/`                                 | `htdocs/_fegg/site/template/cms/tpl/<post type>/`          |
| `code/config/database/` (project tables)                         | `htdocs/_fegg/site/config/database/`                       |
| Project values in `code/config/`                                 | Merged into the skeleton's files in `htdocs/_fegg/site/config/` |
| `code/data/`                                                     | `htdocs/_fegg/site/data/`                                  |
| `htdocs/up_dir/`                                                 | `htdocs/_fegg/site/up_dir/` (served at `/_fegg/site/up_dir/`) |
| The post types in the old CMS menu                               | `htdocs/_fegg/site/config/cms_menu.php`, one block each    |

`fegg/`, the core's libraries in `code/lib/`, the core's CMS screens and templates, `bin/` and `htdocs/cms/` are not brought over: the skeleton has their Fegg 5 versions.

Template paths are relative to `main/tpl/` (see [Sites](../../basic/templates/index.mdx#sites)). In the templates, replace `{{ base }}/data/` with `{{ $site.assets }}/` (`{{ $site.assets }}/css/style.css`, `{{ $site.assets }}/img/...`).

The uploaded files' URL becomes `/_fegg/site/up_dir/...`. Rich text saved in the database still links to `/up_dir/`: replace it in every column that holds rich text, for example:

```sql
UPDATE cms_news SET content = REPLACE(content, '/up_dir/', '/_fegg/site/up_dir/');
```

The database is used as it is: point the project at a copy of the production database and run `./fegg-cli migration`, which only adds the new core tables (such as `rate_limit`).

## 4. Rewrite the PHP

Write each controller again as a namespaced class in `htdocs/_fegg/site/src/Controller/`, replace the Fegg 4 classes with their Fegg 5 names, and add `$this->requirePost()` to every CMS action that changes data. Then go through the behaviour changes: Fegg 5 fails loudly where Fegg 4 was silent.

<SiblingDocCardList ids={["rewrite-php", "behaviour-changes"]} />

## 5. Check

- [ ] `./fegg-cli template:lint` prints OK
- [ ] `php -l` reports no syntax errors for the PHP files under `htdocs/_fegg` (excluding `vendor/` and `data/`)
- [ ] `./fegg-cli migration` against a copy of the production database only adds new core tables such as `rate_limit` (no project table definition is missing, and every YAML file is read: an error names the file and the line)
- [ ] `./fegg-cli core:status` prints `No changes to core files.`
- [ ] The browser console of every CMS screen shows no errors (`$ is not defined` means a template still uses jQuery), and dates (with the time), rich text and gallery ordering are saved
- [ ] Public pages, the contact form (through to sending), CMS login, create / edit / delete for each post type, and media and image uploads work, with the data and uploads of the old site
- [ ] Every post type appears in the CMS side menu and on the dashboard
- [ ] Every saving action in `site/src/Controller/Cms/` calls `$this->requirePost()`: opening its URL in the browser (a GET, e.g. `/cms/news/update/`) answers 405
- [ ] No page links to an action named like a method of `Fegg\Application` (`mail`, `in`, `redirect` ...) or defined only in a base controller (both are 404 now)
- [ ] The PHP error log has no warnings
- [ ] After deploying, `./fegg-cli doctor https://<production URL>` prints OK (`/_fegg/assets/js/base.js` is served; the rest of `/_fegg/`, the sites' SCSS sources included, is not) and the CMS dashboard shows no protection warnings

To roll back, redeploy the old branch. The table definitions are compatible, so the same database works with both.


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/first/migration/rewrite-php ---


# Rewriting the PHP

Write each controller again as a namespaced class in `htdocs/_fegg/site/src/Controller/` (`cms/` becomes `Cms/`). The skeleton's `Cms/News.php` and `Cms/Lab.php` are the models for post types.

```php title="Fegg 4: code/application/cms/News.php"
<?php
class News extends MyApplication
{
    public function __init()
    {
        parent::__init();
        $this->_lh = $this->getClass('LH_FW', 'news');
        $this->_model = new Model(true, array('table' => 'cms_news', 'id' => 'news_id'));
    }
}
```

```php title="Fegg 5: htdocs/_fegg/site/src/Controller/Cms/News.php"
<?php
namespace Site\Controller\Cms;

defined('FEGG_CORE_DIR') || exit;

use Fegg\Model;

class News extends Controller              // Site\Controller\Cms\Controller: the CMS base
{
    protected $_cmsSection = 'news';       // replaces new Helper('news') and the isAuth() check

    public function __init()
    {
        parent::__init();
        $this->_model = new Model(true, array('table' => 'cms_news', 'id' => 'news_id'));
    }

    public function update()
    {
        $this->requirePost();              // required: every action that changes data
        // ...
    }

    public function delete($id)
    {
        $this->requirePost();              // deletes are POST forms with the CSRF token
        // ...
    }
}
```

CMS screens extend `Site\Controller\Cms\Controller`, public pages `Site\Controller\Controller`. The CMS base checks the login and the CSRF token of every POST. Edit and delete forms in the project's CMS templates use `method="post"`: delete links in list templates become POST forms (see [Controller](../../basic/cms/controllers.mdx)). Processing that was added to `MyApplication` goes into `__init()` or a `protected` method of `site/src/Controller/Controller.php`.

:::danger Required: `requirePost()` in every action that changes data

Fegg 4 had no such check. Add `$this->requirePost()` as the first line of every action that saves, deletes, sorts or otherwise writes (`update`, `create`, `delete` ...). The CSRF token is only checked on POST: an action without `requirePost()` still runs on a GET, so a link or an image on another site can change data while a CMS user is logged in. With it, a GET answers 405. Check every action with `grep -n "public function" htdocs/_fegg/site/src/Controller/Cms/*.php`.

:::

| v3 / v4                                    | Fegg 5                                              |
| :----------------------------------------- | :-------------------------------------------------- |
| `MyApplication`                            | `Site\Controller\Controller` (public pages), `Site\Controller\Cms\Controller` (CMS screens) |
| `Model`, `ModelWhere`, `ModelOrder`, ...   | `Fegg\Model`, `Fegg\Model\Where`, `Fegg\Model\Order`, ... |
| `DB_pdo`                                   | `Fegg\Database\Pdo`                                 |
| `$this->getClass('LH_FW', 'news')`         | `new \Fegg\Cms\Helper('news')` (CMS screens get it as `$this->_lh`) |
| `$this->getClass('Validation')`            | `new \Fegg\Tool\Validation()`                       |
| `$this->getClass('Tool/Upload')`, `Tool/File`, `Tool/Mail`, `Tool/RSS`, `Tool/Trim` | `new \Fegg\Tool\Upload()` etc.  |
| `ReCAPTCHA`, `Turnstile`                   | `Fegg\Tool\Captcha\ReCAPTCHA`, `Fegg\Tool\Captcha\Turnstile` |
| `Tool/Date`                                | Removed: PHP's `DateTimeImmutable` (see below)      |
| `Tool/OEmbed`, `core/config/oembed_schemes.php` | Removed: `Fegg\Tool\Embed` (see below)       |
| `$this->getClass('MyLib/Foo')` (`code/lib/`) | `new \Site\MyLib\Foo()` in `site/src/MyLib/Foo.php` |
| `code/lib/Modifire.php`                    | `htdocs/_fegg/site/functions/modifier.php`          |
| `FEGG_CODE_DIR . '/config/...'`, `'/data/...'` | `FEGG_SITE_DIR . '/config/...'`, `'/data/...'`  |
| `FEGG_DIR . '/...'`                        | Not needed (autoloaded)                             |
| `FEGG_TEST_ENVIRONMENT`                    | `FEGG_ENV !== 'production'`                         |

Every file starts with `namespace`, `defined('FEGG_CORE_DIR') || exit;` and `use`. Inside a namespace, prefix PHP's own classes with `\` (`new \Exception`, `new \DateTime`, `\PDO::PARAM_INT`).

- **`Tool/Date`**: `addDay($d, 3)` is `(new \DateTimeImmutable($d))->modify('+3 day')->format('Y-m-d H:i:s')`, `addMinute($d, 30)` is `->modify('+30 minute')`, `getDiff()` is `->diff()`, `getTheLastday()` is `->format('t')`, and a format in a template is `feggDate` (see [Dates and time zone](../../basic/controller.mdx#dates-and-time-zone)). A project that needs `addMonth()` (kept at the end of the month) or `getSocialTime()` copies the Fegg 4 class into `site/src/`.
- **`Tool/OEmbed`**: `(new OEmbed($url))->media` (asked of YouTube) becomes `Fegg\Tool\Embed::youtubeUrl($url)` (a `youtube-nocookie.com` URL, no request), and an `<iframe>` written in a template becomes `{{ $page.data.youtube_url|feggYoutube|noescape }}` / `|feggMap|noescape`, which write the whole block (see [Embed](../../library/embed/index.mdx)). Other oEmbed services: ask them with `Fegg\Http\Client` in the project.

`grep -rn "getClass\|MyApplication\|FEGG_DIR\|FEGG_CODE_DIR\|FEGG_TEST_ENVIRONMENT" htdocs/_fegg/site` must find nothing.


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/first/migration/behaviour-changes ---


# Behaviour changes

Fegg 5 fails loudly where Fegg 4 was silent. The full list is in the 5.0.0 section of `htdocs/_fegg/core/UPGRADE.md`. The ones most likely to affect code brought over:

## Database and models

- **Database errors** throw `Fegg\Database\QueryException` (the message includes the SQL and its parameters) instead of printing and calling `exit`. Uncaught, it is a logged HTTP 500.
- `updateWhere()`, `deleteWhere()` and `eraseWhere()` with an **empty condition** throw instead of affecting every row. Pass `'1 = 1'` where every row was really meant.
- `Model::order()`, `join()` and the `table` / `id` / `visible` options accept **only plain names**. Move expressions to `orderRaw()`.
- **`OR` in conditions** is bracketed: `where('category = ?', 1)->where('a = ? OR b = ?', ...)` is `category = 1 AND (a = ? OR b = ?)`, and the visible flag and `valid = 1` are added outside the brackets. In Fegg 4 such conditions matched rows of every category, and `where('a = ?', 1)->where('b = ?', 2, 'OR')` also returned unpublished and deleted rows matching `b`; such lists can get shorter.

## Templates

- **Missing templates**, `include` targets and `transclude` parents throw `Fegg\Template\TemplateException` (HTTP 500) instead of printing "Can't Read" into the page. `./fegg-cli template:lint` lists them all.
- **Template blocks are checked**: an unclosed block or an extra `{{ end }}` throws with the line number.
- **Sections** are passed to the parent template as closures. Code that called the global `section_<name>()` functions directly must be rewritten.
- `{{ options }}` escapes labels and values, and `{{ hidden }}` escapes values.
- **Arrays reach modifiers**: `{{ $tags|feggImplode:', ' }}` now prints the joined tags (it printed nothing).

## Requests, sessions and security

- **Actions**: only a controller's own public methods can be called from a URL (see [Controller](../../basic/controller.mdx#which-methods-are-actions)). An action defined in `MyApplication` or a base controller is a 404: write it in each controller that uses it. An action named like a method of `Fegg\Application` (`mail`, `in`, `redirect` ...) is a 404: rename it, or map the old URL with a route.
- **Basic authentication** with `basic_auth_scope = 1` is checked before the controller's `__init()` and action, so redirects, downloads and direct output are protected too.
- **No more `exit`**: Fegg ends responses by throwing `Fegg\Http\Halt`; rethrow it from a `catch (\Throwable $e)` and replace your own `exit` with `$this->halt()` (see [Ending a response](../../basic/controller.mdx#ending-a-response)).
- **Sessions and cookies**: a session starts only when something is written to it (see [Session](../../basic/parameters/index.mdx#session)). `setCookie()` takes SameSite as its own argument (not `'/; SameSite=Lax'` in the path), and `unsetCookie()` really deletes the cookie (Fegg 4 set an empty cookie for 7 days; see [setCookie](../../basic/parameters/cookie/setcookie.mdx)).
- **Security headers** are sent with every response; a page framed by another site calls `$this->allowFraming()` (see [Security](../../basic/security.mdx#security-headers)).
- **HTTPS** is detected from more server variables and proxy headers, and `redirect()` no longer sends HTTPS pages to HTTP when the detection fails (see [Security](../../basic/security.mdx#https)).
- **Route-only controllers**: controllers that implement `Fegg\Http\RouteOnly` (such as the skeleton's `Site\Controller\Page`) are only reached through `site/config/route.php`. Add a route such as `$route['about/'] = 'page/render/about';` for each static page that was reached as `/page/render/...` (see [URL](../../basic/url.mdx#static-pages)).
- **`htdocs/.htaccess`**: remove the `<IfModule mod_php5.c>` block and the Apache 2.2 `Order` / `Deny from all` lines (500 on Apache 2.4), and add the dotfile rules and the `_fegg` exceptions (the CMS's files, the site folders, the uploads) of `core/dist/htaccess.dist`. On nginx, use the rules of [Directory structure](../cont.mdx#protecting-_fegg). `fegg-cli doctor` warns about the old lines.
- **Captcha keys and the database DSN** move to `env.php`: the skeleton's `database_config.php` and `contact/contact_config.php` read them with `feggEnv()`. Put the project's keys there, not in committed files.

## CMS

- **CMS sessions** need the session's user to still exist in `cms_user`: deleting a user ends their sessions.
- **CMS login**: logout is a POST. Copied CMS frame templates in `site/template/cms/tpl/` add the class `js-cms-logout` to the logout link. Run `fegg-cli migration` for the login throttling table `rate_limit`. `/cms/login/create/...` is gone: use `fegg-cli user:create` (see [fegg-cli](../cli.mdx#cms-users)).
- **The CMS's files** are in `htdocs/_fegg/assets/`, read at `/_fegg/assets/`. Copies of CMS templates in `site/template/cms/tpl/` load them with `{{ $site.cms_assets }}` instead of `{{ $site.cms_dir }}` (`css/`, `js/`, `img/`, `vendor/`). `_fegg/` can no longer be outside the document root. See [CMS screens and the editor](../../basic/cms/index.mdx).
- **No jQuery in the CMS** (nor jQuery UI, Select2 or the datetimepicker). Rewrite `$(...)` in the project's CMS templates (`site/template/cms/tpl/*/html_head.tpl`) with plain JavaScript and the `FeggCms` helpers, or load a jQuery of your own there. Date fields become `datetime-local` inputs and drag & drop ordering uses SortableJS (see [Dates](../../basic/cms/index.mdx#dates) and [Drag and drop ordering](../../basic/cms/index.mdx#drag-and-drop-ordering)).
- **CKEditor 4 is removed.** Rich text fields use FeggEditor: replace `CKEDITOR.replace(...)` and `ckeditor/ckeditor.js` with `data-fegg-editor="full"` on the textarea and `js/editor.js`, and clean the saved HTML with `Fegg\Cms\HtmlSanitizer::clean()`. Text written with CKEditor opens in FeggEditor. Copied `cms/image/` and `cms/media/` templates use the new picker scripts (`js/modal/image_picker.js`, `file_picker.js`; parameters `from=wysiwyg&picker=<id>`).
- **Font Awesome 4.3 is removed.** Icons are Tabler Icons: replace `fa fa-…` with `ti ti-…`: `search` → `search`, `angle-right` → `chevron-right`, `image` / `picture-o` → `photo`, `times` → `x`, `cog` → `settings` (`pencil`, `trash`, `plus`, `link`, `check`, `download`, `upload` keep their names; others: tabler.io/icons). An icon the core does not use needs a line in `unique.scss` (see [Icons](../../basic/cms/index.mdx#icons)). Copies of `cms/frame/sitewire.tpl` and `popframe.tpl` load `vendor/tabler-icons/icons.css` instead of `fonts/font-awesome.min.css`.

## Libraries

- **Uploads** check the content against the extension; add the `@content` message to a copied `site/config/file_error_message.php` (see [Upload](../../library/upload/configuration.mdx)).
- **Spyc is removed.** YAML is read by `Fegg\Tool\Yaml` (see [Yaml](../../library/yaml/index.mdx)): replace `spyc_load_file()` / `Spyc::YAMLLoad()` in project code with `Fegg\Tool\Yaml::parseFile()`. Table definitions read as before; `yes` / `no` and numbers with a leading zero are now text, and anchors or tags are refused with the file and the line.
- **`getDatetime()` / `convertDatetime()`** no longer change the process-wide time zone, and `convertDatetime()` returns `''` for a date it cannot read (it returned `1970-01-01 ...`).
- **Images in other sizes** (`getImageUrl`) are limited by `image_max` and `image_cache_max` in `site/config/upload_dir.php`, and the positions `lb` and `rb` now keep the bottom left and the bottom right (see [Trim](../../library/trim/index.mdx#images-of-the-cms-in-other-sizes)).
- **`Fegg\Tool\Trim`** throws a `\RuntimeException` for a missing file, a file that is not a JPEG, PNG or GIF, or another output type (it called `exit`).
- **`Fegg\Tool\File`** methods return `true` / `false`. `removeDirectory()` removes symbolic links, never what they point to.
- **`Fegg\Tool\Upload::upload()`** for a field the form does not have is `@empty` (or OK when not required), and several files (`name="file[]"`) are `@failed`, without PHP warnings.
- **`Fegg\Tool\Mail`**: the debug log is `mail.log` in the log directory (it was `/tmp/sendmaillog`). `send()` without a sender uses `$settings['mail']['from']` / `['from_name']`, or throws an `\InvalidArgumentException`.
- **`Fegg\Tool\RSS`** throws a `\RuntimeException` for a feed it cannot fetch or read, and keeps posts whose title contains "PR:": pass `array('skip_pr' => true)` to leave them out as before.

## Additional work for v3 projects

- **Mail** (`Fegg\Tool\Mail`) uses PHPMailer. `sendWithFile()`, `setInputCharset()` and `setSendCharset()` were removed: add attachments with `pushAttached()` and call `send()`. A failed send throws `PHPMailer\PHPMailer\Exception`.
- **Trailing slash**: URLs without an extension are redirected (301) to the same URL with a trailing `/`. Check the keys in `route.php` and the links in templates.
- **PHP 8**: fix deprecation warnings in the project code (for example passing `null` to string functions).


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library ---


# About Extension libraries

Fegg's goal is lightweight and less typing, so the controller base class only has the minimum required functions. Additional functions are provided as library classes in the core (`htdocs/_fegg/core/src/`).

## How to use

Every library is a namespaced class that is loaded by the autoloader. Import it with `use` and create it with `new`.

```php title="Controller"
<?php
namespace Site\Controller;

defined('FEGG_CORE_DIR') || exit;

use Fegg\Tool\Mail;
use Fegg\Tool\Validation;

class Contact extends Controller
{
    public function post()
    {
        $validation = new Validation();
        $validation->required('mail', $this->in('mail'), array('@required', 'Email'));

        if (!$validation->isError()) {
            $mail = new Mail('Mail subject', 'Mail body');
            $mail->send($this->in('mail'), 'Sender name', 'from@example.com');
        }
    }
}
```

## Extension Libraries

The libraries are in the `Fegg\Tool\` namespace. `Fegg\Model`, `Fegg\Database\Pdo`, `Fegg\Http\Client` and `Fegg\Log` are framework classes, not in `Fegg\Tool\`.

| Library | Class | Description |
| --- | --- | --- |
| [Validation](./validation/index.mdx) | `Fegg\Tool\Validation` | Form validation |
| [Database](./database/index.mdx) | `Fegg\Database\Pdo` | Database access (PDO) |
| [Model](./model/index.mdx) | `Fegg\Model` | Models: queries and rows |
| [File](./file/index.mdx) | `Fegg\Tool\File` | File and directory operations |
| [Upload](./upload/index.mdx) | `Fegg\Tool\Upload` | Uploads and file checks |
| [Mail](./mail/index.mdx) | `Fegg\Tool\Mail` | Sending mail (PHPMailer) |
| [RSS](./rss/index.mdx) | `Fegg\Tool\RSS` | Reading RSS / Atom feeds |
| [Trim](./trim/index.mdx) | `Fegg\Tool\Trim` | Resizing and cropping images |
| [Embed](./embed/index.mdx) | `Fegg\Tool\Embed` | Embedding YouTube videos and Google Maps the way the editor does |
| [Captcha](./captcha/index.mdx) | `Fegg\Tool\Captcha\ReCAPTCHA`, `Fegg\Tool\Captcha\Turnstile` | Verifying Google reCAPTCHA / Cloudflare Turnstile responses |
| [Yaml](./yaml/index.mdx) | `Fegg\Tool\Yaml` | Reading and writing YAML files |
| [HTTP client](./http-client/index.mdx) | `Fegg\Http\Client` | Requests to other services over HTTP |
| [Log](./log/index.mdx) | `Fegg\Log` | Writing to the application log |
| [Cache](./cache/index.mdx) | `Fegg\Tool\Cache` | Keeping values in files for a while |
| [CSV](./csv/index.mdx) | `Fegg\Tool\Csv` | Writing and reading CSV for Excel |
| [Rate limit](./rate-limit/index.mdx) | `Fegg\Tool\RateLimit` | Limiting how often one visitor can do something |
| [Sitemap](./sitemap/index.mdx) | `Fegg\Tool\Sitemap` | Writing sitemap.xml |
| [JSON-LD](./json-ld/index.mdx) | `Fegg\Tool\JsonLd` | Structured data for search engines |
| [Feed](./feed/index.mdx) | `Fegg\Tool\Feed` | Writing an RSS 2.0 feed of the site's posts |

## Project libraries

Put your own classes in `htdocs/_fegg/site/src/` with the `Site\` namespace. They are autoloaded in the same way.

```php title="htdocs/_fegg/site/src/MyLib/Foo.php"
<?php
namespace Site\MyLib;

defined('FEGG_CORE_DIR') || exit;

class Foo
{
}
```

```php title="Controller"
use Site\MyLib\Foo;

$foo = new Foo();
```

## External Libraries

These libraries are bundled in `htdocs/_fegg/core/vendor/` and autoloaded.

- [PHPMailer](https://github.com/PHPMailer/PHPMailer) (7.2)
  - Used by [Mail](./mail/index.mdx). Namespace `PHPMailer\PHPMailer`
  - A failed send throws `PHPMailer\PHPMailer\Exception`

JSON documents are handled with PHP's own `json_encode` and `json_decode`, YAML with [Yaml](./yaml/index.mdx).


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/cache ---


import { CategoryDocCardList } from '@site/src/components/CategoryDocCardList';

# Introduction

> Keeping values in files for a while

`Fegg\Tool\Cache` keeps a value for a number of seconds, so the next requests do not compute it again: the result of another service (a feed, an API) or of a slow query. Each key is one file in `$settings['cache_dir']`.

## How to use

The methods are static: import `Fegg\Tool\Cache` and call [remember()](./methods/remember.mdx) with a key, the seconds to keep the value and a function that returns it.

```php title="Controller"
<?php
namespace Site\Controller;

defined('FEGG_CORE_DIR') || exit;

use Fegg\Tool\Cache;
use Fegg\Tool\RSS;

class Index extends Controller
{
    public function index()
    {
        try {
            // Fetched at most once every 10 minutes
            $this->page['posts'] = Cache::remember('blog-feed', 600, function () {
                return (new RSS('https://blog.example.com/feed'))->fetchAll(5);
            });
        } catch (\RuntimeException $e) {
            $this->page['posts'] = array();
        }

        $this->displayPage('index');
    }
}
```

Put everything the value depends on in the key: `'items-' . $category`. Keys are any text; the file name is made from a hash of the key.

## When the function fails

When the value has expired and the function throws, `remember()` returns the **expired value** if it is still there and writes a warning with the key and the exception to the [log](../log/index.mdx) (`Cache: expired value of blog-feed used, the new one failed: ...`). The page keeps working while the other service is down. Without an expired value, the exception is thrown.

The value is kept again only when the function succeeds, so the next request tries again. `Fegg\Http\Halt` (a redirect inside the function) is never caught.

## Values

A value can contain:

- arrays, strings, numbers, booleans and `null`;
- dates: `\DateTime` and `\DateTimeImmutable` (the posts of [RSS](../rss/index.mdx) have them).

Any other object throws `\InvalidArgumentException`, also inside an array: a cache file can then never create an object when it is read. Keep model rows (arrays) or convert objects to arrays first.

## Files

| Setting | Default | Remarks |
| :------ | :------ | :------ |
| `$settings['cache_dir']` | `site/data/cache/data` | Directory of the files (created when missing) |

- A file is written to a temporary name and then renamed, so a request never reads a half-written file.
- An expired file stays until it is written again or removed with `fegg-cli cache:clear`; it is treated as missing (except by the fallback above).
- A file that cannot be read is treated as missing.
- When the directory or the file cannot be written, a warning goes to the log and the value is not kept: `remember()` still returns it.
- `site/data/` is not tracked by git.

## Clearing

`fegg-cli cache:clear` removes every value, for example after a deploy that changes what a value contains:

```bash
./fegg-cli cache:clear
# OK: 3 value(s) removed from /path/to/htdocs/_fegg/site/data/cache/data
```

[Cache::delete()](./methods/delete.mdx) removes one key, for example when the CMS saves the data the value was made from.

## Errors

- A ttl below 1 second, or a value with an object other than a date, throws `\InvalidArgumentException`.
- What the function of `remember()` throws is thrown again when there is no expired value (see [When the function fails](#when-the-function-fails)).
- A directory or file that cannot be written does not throw (see [Files](#files)).

## Methods

<CategoryDocCardList label="Methods" />


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/cache/methods/remember ---


# Cache::remember

> Return the kept value, or make it and keep it

## Description

```php
public static mixed Cache::remember( string $key, int $ttl, callable $callback )
```

Returns the value of `$key` when it has not expired. Otherwise calls `$callback`, keeps its result for `$ttl` seconds and returns it.

When `$callback` throws and an expired value is still there, that value is returned and a warning is logged; without one, the exception is thrown (see [When the function fails](../index.mdx#when-the-function-fails)).

A result with an object other than a date throws `\InvalidArgumentException` (see [Values](../index.mdx#values)).

## Arguments

| Name      | Data type | Default | Remarks |
| --------- | --------- | ------- | ------- |
| $key      | string    |         | Name of the value |
| $ttl      | int       |         | Seconds to keep it (at least 1) |
| $callback | callable  |         | Returns the value |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Http\Client;
use Fegg\Tool\Cache;

$category = (int) $this->in('category');

$items = Cache::remember('items-' . $category, 300, function () use ($category) {
    $response = (new Client(5))->get('https://api.example.com/items?category=' . $category);
    if ($response['status'] !== 200) {
        throw new \RuntimeException('Items API answered ' . $response['status']);
    }
    return json_decode($response['body'], true);
});
```

Throw when the result is not usable: an error status returned as a value would be kept.


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/cache/methods/get ---


# Cache::get

> Read a kept value

## Description

```php
public static mixed Cache::get( string $key [, mixed $default = null ] )
```

Returns the value of `$key`, or `$default` when there is none or it has expired.

## Arguments

| Name     | Data type | Default | Remarks |
| -------- | --------- | ------- | ------- |
| $key     | string    |         | Name of the value |
| $default | mixed     | `null`  | Returned when there is no value |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Tool\Cache;

$ranking = Cache::get('ranking', array());
```

A kept `null` cannot be told apart from a missing value: use [has()](./has.mdx) for that.


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/cache/methods/has ---


# Cache::has

> Check for a kept value

## Description

```php
public static bool Cache::has( string $key )
```

Returns `true` when `$key` has a value that has not expired.

## Arguments

| Name | Data type | Default | Remarks |
| ---- | --------- | ------- | ------- |
| $key | string    |         | Name of the value |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Tool\Cache;

if (!Cache::has('ranking')) {
    Cache::set('ranking', $this->_buildRanking(), 3600);
}
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/cache/methods/set ---


# Cache::set

> Keep a value

## Description

```php
public static bool Cache::set( string $key, mixed $value, int $ttl )
```

Keeps `$value` for `$ttl` seconds, replacing the value of `$key`. The file is written to a temporary name and then renamed.

Returns `false` when the directory or the file cannot be written (a warning is logged).

Throws `\InvalidArgumentException` when `$ttl` is below 1 or `$value` contains an object other than `\DateTime` / `\DateTimeImmutable` (see [Values](../index.mdx#values)).

## Arguments

| Name   | Data type | Default | Remarks |
| ------ | --------- | ------- | ------- |
| $key   | string    |         | Name of the value |
| $value | mixed     |         | Arrays, scalars, `null` and dates |
| $ttl   | int       |         | Seconds to keep it (at least 1) |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Tool\Cache;

Cache::set('ranking', array(
    'items'   => $items,
    'updated' => new \DateTimeImmutable(),
), 3600);
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/cache/methods/delete ---


# Cache::delete

> Remove one value

## Description

```php
public static void Cache::delete( string $key )
```

Removes the file of `$key`. Nothing happens when there is none.

## Arguments

| Name | Data type | Default | Remarks |
| ---- | --------- | ------- | ------- |
| $key | string    |         | Name of the value |

## Example

```php title="CMS controller"
use Fegg\Tool\Cache;

public function update($id)
{
    $this->requirePost();
    // ... save the post
    Cache::delete('news-latest');   // the top page shows the new post at once
    $this->redirect($this->page['prefix'] . '/index/UPDATED');
}
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/cache/methods/clear ---


# Cache::clear

> Remove every value

## Description

```php
public static int Cache::clear()
```

Removes every cache file of the [directory](./directory.mdx) and returns how many were removed. `fegg-cli cache:clear` calls it.

## Example

```php
use Fegg\Tool\Cache;

$count = Cache::clear();
```

```bash title="The same from the command line"
./fegg-cli cache:clear
# OK: 3 value(s) removed from /path/to/htdocs/_fegg/site/data/cache/data
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/cache/methods/directory ---


# Cache::directory / Cache::setDirectory

> The directory of the files, and replacing it in tests

## Description

```php
public static string Cache::directory()
public static void Cache::setDirectory( ?string $directory )
```

`directory()` returns the directory of the cache files: `$settings['cache_dir']`, or `site/data/cache/data` when the setting is empty.

`setDirectory()` uses another directory for the rest of the request, for tests. `null` reads the setting again.

## Arguments

| Name       | Data type    | Default | Remarks |
| ---------- | ------------ | ------- | ------- |
| $directory | string\|null |         | Directory (a trailing `/` is removed), or `null` for the setting |

## Example

```php title="Test"
use Fegg\Tool\Cache;

protected function setUp(): void
{
    Cache::setDirectory(sys_get_temp_dir() . '/fegg-cache-test');
}

protected function tearDown(): void
{
    Cache::clear();
    Cache::setDirectory(null);
}
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/captcha ---


import { CategoryDocCardList } from '@site/src/components/CategoryDocCardList';

# Introduction

> Verifying Google reCAPTCHA / Cloudflare Turnstile responses

Two classes verify the token posted by a CAPTCHA widget with the service's API:

- `Fegg\Tool\Captcha\ReCAPTCHA`: [Google reCAPTCHA v3](https://developers.google.com/recaptcha/docs/v3) (score based)
- `Fegg\Tool\Captcha\Turnstile`: [Cloudflare Turnstile](https://developers.cloudflare.com/turnstile/)

Both send the token with [Fegg\Http\Client](../http-client/index.mdx) (5 second timeout).

## How to use

Import the class and create it with `new`, passing the secret key. Then pass the posted token to `auth()`.

```php title="Controller"
<?php
namespace Site\Controller;

defined('FEGG_CORE_DIR') || exit;

use Fegg\Tool\Captcha\ReCAPTCHA;
use Fegg\Tool\Captcha\Turnstile;
use Fegg\Tool\Validation;

class Entry extends Controller
{
    public function confirm()
    {
        $validation = new Validation();

        // reCAPTCHA (the secret comes from site/config/env.php)
        $recaptcha = new ReCAPTCHA(feggEnv('FEGG_RECAPTCHA_SECRET', ''), 0.5);
        $result = $recaptcha->auth($this->in('g-recaptcha-response'));
        if (!$result['success']) {
            $validation->setError('g-recaptcha-response', implode(',', $result['errors']));
        }

        // Cloudflare Turnstile
        $turnstile = new Turnstile(feggEnv('FEGG_TURNSTILE_SECRET', ''));
        $result = $turnstile->auth($this->in('cf-turnstile-response'));
        if (!$result['success']) {
            $validation->setError('cf-turnstile-response', implode(',', $result['errors']));
        }
    }
}
```

## When the service cannot be reached

On a timeout, an outage or an answer that is not HTTP 200 JSON, `auth()` fails: `success` is `false` and `errors` has a message asking the visitor to try again later (`確認サービスに接続できませんでした。…`). A warning is written to the [application log](../../basic/logging.mdx). A form protected by a captcha is never accepted unchecked.

## Keys

The keys differ per server, and the secret keys must not be committed. Put them in `site/config/env.php` (or environment variables) and read them with `feggEnv()`:

```php title="htdocs/_fegg/site/config/env.php"
'FEGG_RECAPTCHA_SITE_KEY' => '...',
'FEGG_RECAPTCHA_SECRET'   => '...',
'FEGG_TURNSTILE_SITE_KEY' => '...',
'FEGG_TURNSTILE_SECRET'   => '...',
```

Use different keys (or the providers' test keys) for development and production. On a test server the keys can go in the committed `site/config/env/staging.php` (see [Test servers deployed with git pull](../../first/config/environments.mdx#test-servers-deployed-with-git-pull)):

| | Site key | Secret |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| Turnstile, always passes | `1x00000000000000000000AA` | `1x0000000000000000000000000000000AA` |
| Turnstile, always fails | `2x00000000000000000000AB` | `2x0000000000000000000000000000000AA` |

Test secrets accept only the dummy token of the test site keys ([Cloudflare](https://developers.cloudflare.com/turnstile/troubleshooting/testing/)); never use them in production. reCAPTCHA v3 has no test keys: register the test server's domain, and set `FEGG_RECAPTCHA_SCORE_THRESHOLD` to `1.0` to see a rejection. When a captcha is enabled but a key is empty, every form is rejected and an error is logged.

## Contact form

The contact form of the skeleton supports both. Enable one in `htdocs/_fegg/site/config/contact/contact_config.php` with `$_enable_recaptcha` or `$_enable_turnstile`; the file reads the keys from `feggEnv()`:

```php title="htdocs/_fegg/site/config/contact/contact_config.php"
$_enable_recaptcha = true;
$_recaptcha_key    = feggEnv('FEGG_RECAPTCHA_SITE_KEY', '');
$_recaptcha_secret = feggEnv('FEGG_RECAPTCHA_SECRET', '');
$_recaptcha_score_threshold = 0.5;
```

The widgets are output by `site/template/main/tpl/template/antispam.tpl`, and `site/src/Controller/Contact.php` verifies them.

## Methods

<CategoryDocCardList label="Methods" />


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/captcha/methods/recaptcha-construct ---


# ReCAPTCHA::\_\_construct

> Set the reCAPTCHA secret key and score threshold

## Description

```php
public ReCAPTCHA::__construct( string $secret, [ float $threshold = 0.5 ] )
```

## Arguments

| Name       | Data type | Default | Remarks                                                                 |
| ---------- | --------- | ------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| $secret    | string    |         | reCAPTCHA secret key. Read it from `site/config/env.php` with `feggEnv('FEGG_RECAPTCHA_SECRET')` |
| $threshold | float     | 0.5     | Minimum score (0.0 to 1.0) / a lower score fails [ReCAPTCHA::auth](./recaptcha-auth.mdx) |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Tool\Captcha\ReCAPTCHA;

$recaptcha = new ReCAPTCHA(feggEnv('FEGG_RECAPTCHA_SECRET', ''), 0.5);
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/captcha/methods/recaptcha-auth ---


# ReCAPTCHA::auth

> Verify a reCAPTCHA token

## Description

```php
public array ReCAPTCHA::auth( string $response, [ float $threshold = null ] )
```

Sends the token to `https://www.google.com/recaptcha/api/siteverify` and returns an array:

| Key     | Data type | Remarks                                                                  |
| ------- | --------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| success | boolean   | `true` when the API returned success, the score is not below the threshold and there is no error |
| errors  | array     | Error messages (English for the API error codes; a Japanese message when the score is below the threshold) |

When the service cannot be reached, `success` is `false` (see [When the service cannot be reached](../index.mdx#when-the-service-cannot-be-reached)).

## Arguments

| Name      | Data type | Default | Remarks                                              |
| --------- | --------- | ------- | ---------------------------------------------------- |
| $response | string    |         | Token posted by the widget (`g-recaptcha-response`)   |
| $threshold | float    | null    | Minimum score for this call. `null`: the threshold given to the constructor |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Tool\Captcha\ReCAPTCHA;

$recaptcha = new ReCAPTCHA(feggEnv('FEGG_RECAPTCHA_SECRET', ''), 0.5);
$result = $recaptcha->auth($this->in('g-recaptcha-response'));

if (!$result['success']) {
    $errorMessage = implode(',', $result['errors']);
}
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/captcha/methods/turnstile-construct ---

## Example

```php title="Controller"

$turnstile = new Turnstile(feggEnv('FEGG_TURNSTILE_SECRET', ''));
```

# Turnstile::auth

> Verify a Turnstile token

## Description

```php
public array Turnstile::auth( string $response )
```

Sends the token to `https://challenges.cloudflare.com/turnstile/v0/siteverify` and returns an array:

| Key     | Data type | Remarks                                    |
| ------- | --------- | ------------------------------------------ |
| success | boolean   | `true` when the API returned success       |
| errors  | array     | Error messages for the API error codes     |

When the service cannot be reached, `success` is `false` (see [When the service cannot be reached](../index.mdx#when-the-service-cannot-be-reached)).

## Arguments

| Name      | Data type | Default | Remarks                                              |
| --------- | --------- | ------- | ---------------------------------------------------- |
| $response | string    |         | Token posted by the widget (`cf-turnstile-response`)  |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Tool\Captcha\Turnstile;

$turnstile = new Turnstile(feggEnv('FEGG_TURNSTILE_SECRET', ''));
$result = $turnstile->auth($this->in('cf-turnstile-response'));

if (!$result['success']) {
    $errorMessage = implode(',', $result['errors']);
}
```
