import { CategoryDocCardList } from '@site/src/components/CategoryDocCardList';

# Introduction

> Writing and reading CSV for Excel

`Fegg\Tool\Csv` writes CSV that Excel opens without garbled text (a download from the CMS) and reads CSV back (an import), including files saved by Excel in Shift_JIS.

## How to use

The methods are static: import `Fegg\Tool\Csv` and call them. A CMS action that downloads a list:

```php title="htdocs/_fegg/site/src/Controller/Cms/Entry.php"
<?php
namespace Site\Controller\Cms;

defined('FEGG_CORE_DIR') || exit;

use Fegg\Model;
use Fegg\Tool\Csv;

class Entry extends Controller
{
    protected $_cmsSection = 'entry';

    public function __init()
    {
        parent::__init();
        $this->_model = new Model(true, array('table' => 'cms_entry', 'id' => 'entry_id'));
    }

    public function export()
    {
        // Rows are written one at a time
        $rows = function () {
            foreach ($this->_model->order('created', 'desc')->all() as $row) {
                yield array($row['name'], $row['mail'], $row['created']);
            }
        };
        Csv::download('entries.csv', $rows(), array('header' => array('名前', 'メール', '日時')));
    }
}
```

[download()](./methods/download.mdx) sends the file and ends the response, like `displayPage()`. [toString()](./methods/tostring.mdx) returns the same text instead.

## Writing

Lines end with CRLF, and a field is quoted when it contains `,`, `"` or a line break.

| `'encoding'` | Written as |
| :----------- | :--------- |
| `'utf-8-bom'` (default) | UTF-8 with a BOM: Excel opens it without garbled text |
| `'utf-8'` | UTF-8 without a BOM, for other programs |
| `'sjis'` | Shift_JIS (Windows characters included), for older tools |

Values are written as text: `null` is empty, `true` / `false` are `1` / `0`, dates (`\DateTimeInterface`) are `Y-m-d H:i:s`. An array as a field throws `\InvalidArgumentException`. Only the values of a row are written, in their order: the keys are ignored.

### Formulas

Text that starts with `=`, `+`, `-`, `@`, a tab or a CR gets a leading `'`, so a spreadsheet shows what a visitor typed instead of running it as a formula ("CSV injection"). Numbers are not changed: `-5` (an int, or the string `"-5"` read from the database) is written as `-5`.

```text title="Written"
'=1+2
-5
"'=HYPERLINK(""http://example.com"",""Click"")"
```

`'escape_formulas' => false` writes the text as it is; use it only for data no visitor can type.

## Reading

[parse()](./methods/parse.mdx) reads CSV text, [parseFile()](./methods/parsefile.mdx) a file and [parseUpload()](./methods/parseupload.mdx) a file sent with a form.

- UTF-8 (with or without a BOM) and Shift_JIS are detected: the text is UTF-8 when it is valid UTF-8, otherwise Shift_JIS. `'encoding' => 'utf-8'` or `'sjis'` sets it.
- Values are strings and are not trimmed. A field in quotes can span several lines.
- Empty lines are skipped.

**Rows are keyed by their row number, as a spreadsheet shows it**: the header is row 1, and a field over several lines is one row. An error message can name the row the user sees. Since empty lines are skipped, the keys can jump: `array_values($rows)` makes a list.

### Header and columns

| Option | Rows |
| :----- | :--- |
| none | Lists of fields: `array(2 => array('Hello', 'news'), ...)` |
| `'header' => true` | Keyed by the titles of the first row: `array(2 => array('タイトル' => 'Hello', 'カテゴリー' => 'news'), ...)` |
| `'header' => true, 'columns' => array('タイトル' => 'title', ...)` | Keyed by your names (`array(2 => array('title' => 'Hello'), ...)`). Other columns are left out, the order of the columns does not matter, and a missing title is an error |

With `'header' => true`, a row with another number of fields than the header throws `\RuntimeException` naming the row (`entries.csv: row 5 has 3 fields, the header has 4`).

## Importing a file

A CMS form sends the file (`<form method="post" enctype="multipart/form-data">` with `<input type="file" name="csv">` and the CSRF token). Check every row first, and save only when all rows are right:

```php title="htdocs/_fegg/site/src/Controller/Cms/News.php"
use Fegg\Tool\Csv;
use Fegg\Tool\Validation;

public function import()
{
    $this->requirePost();

    // The titles the export writes, and the columns they go to
    $columns = array('タイトル' => 'title', 'カテゴリー' => 'category', '投稿日' => 'post_date');
    try {
        $rows = Csv::parseUpload('csv', array('header' => true, 'columns' => $columns));
    } catch (\RuntimeException $e) {
        $this->page['error'] = array($e->getMessage());   // no file, too large, a missing column ...
        $this->displayPage($this->page['prefix'] . '/import');
    }

    $errors = Csv::rowErrors($rows, function (array $row) {
        $validation = new Validation();
        $validation->required('title', $row['title'], array('@required', 'タイトル'));
        $validation->datetime('post_date', $row['post_date'], array('@date', '投稿日'));
        return $validation->getErrorMessage();
    });
    if ($errors) {
        // "3行目: ...": nothing is saved until every row is right
        $this->page['error'] = array();
        foreach ($errors as $line => $messages) {
            $this->page['error'][] = $line . '行目: ' . implode(' ', $messages);
        }
        $this->displayPage($this->page['prefix'] . '/import');
    }

    $this->_model->insertRows(array_values($rows));   // one transaction: all rows or none
    $this->redirect($this->page['prefix'] . '/index/IMPORTED');
}
```

A file that was exported keeps the `'` added before formulas: remove it (`ltrim($value, "'")`) in columns that can never start with `'`. Rich text columns go through `HtmlSanitizer::clean()` as in the edit form.

## Errors

- A file that cannot be read, a broken row, a missing column, or an upload that is missing, failed, too large or not `.csv` / `.txt` throws `\RuntimeException`. The message names the file and, for a row, its number: it can be shown to the CMS user.
- An unknown encoding, `'columns'` without `'header' => true` or an array as a field throws `\InvalidArgumentException` (a mistake in the code).

## Methods

<CategoryDocCardList label="Methods" />


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/csv/methods/tostring ---


# Csv::toString

> Return rows as CSV text

## Description

```php
public static string Csv::toString( iterable $rows [, array $options = array() ] )
```

Returns the CSV text of `$rows`, with a BOM first for `'utf-8-bom'` (see [Writing](../index.mdx#writing)).

## Arguments

| Name     | Data type | Default   | Remarks |
| -------- | --------- | --------- | ------- |
| $rows    | iterable  |           | Rows: arrays (lists or associative; only the values are written), or a generator |
| $options | array     | `array()` | See below |

### Options

| Key             | Default       | Remarks |
| --------------- | ------------- | ------- |
| header          | `array()`     | Titles written as the first row |
| encoding        | `'utf-8-bom'` | `'utf-8-bom'`, `'utf-8'` or `'sjis'` |
| escape_formulas | `true`        | `false` writes text starting with `=` `+` `-` `@` as it is (see [Formulas](../index.mdx#formulas)) |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Tool\Csv;

$csv = Csv::toString(array(
    array('Hello, world', 3, true, null),
    array('=SUM(A1:A2)', -5, false, new \DateTime('2026-11-15 10:00')),
), array('header' => array('Text', 'Number', 'Flag', 'Date'), 'encoding' => 'utf-8'));
```

```text title="$csv"
Text,Number,Flag,Date
"Hello, world",3,1,
'=SUM(A1:A2),-5,0,2026-11-15 10:00:00
```

Use it to attach a CSV to a mail or save it to a file; [download()](./download.mdx) sends it to the browser.


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/csv/methods/download ---


# Csv::download

> Send rows as a CSV download and end the response

## Description

```php
public static void Csv::download( string $fileName, iterable $rows [, array $options = array() ] )
```

Sends `$rows` as a CSV file to save and ends the response (it throws `Fegg\Http\Halt`, like `displayPage()`). Code after it does not run.

Rows are written one at a time and the output is not buffered: pass a generator to send many rows without holding them all in memory. The response is sent with `Content-Type: text/csv` (charset `UTF-8` or `Shift_JIS`) and `Cache-Control: private, no-store`.

## Arguments

| Name      | Data type | Default   | Remarks |
| --------- | --------- | --------- | ------- |
| $fileName | string    |           | Name in the browser's save dialog (Japanese is fine) |
| $rows     | iterable  |           | Rows, as for [toString()](./tostring.mdx) |
| $options  | array     | `array()` | `header`, `encoding`, `escape_formulas`: see [toString()](./tostring.mdx#options) |

## Example

```php title="CMS controller"
use Fegg\Tool\Csv;

public function export()
{
    $rows = function () {
        foreach ($this->_model->order('post_date', 'desc')->all() as $row) {
            yield array($row['title'], $row['category'], $row['post_date']);
        }
    };
    Csv::download('ニュース_' . date('Ymd') . '.csv', $rows(), array(
        'header' => array('タイトル', 'カテゴリー', '投稿日'),
    ));
}
```

Write the same titles that the import expects in `'columns'`, so an exported file can be edited and sent back (see [Importing a file](../index.mdx#importing-a-file)).


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/csv/methods/parse ---


# Csv::parse

> Read CSV text

## Description

```php
public static array Csv::parse( string $text [, array $options = array() [, string $source = 'CSV' ] ] )
```

Returns the rows of `$text`, keyed by their row number (see [Reading](../index.mdx#reading)). Values are strings.

Throws `\RuntimeException` when, with `'header'`, a row has another number of fields than the header or a title of `'columns'` is missing. The message starts with `$source`.

## Arguments

| Name     | Data type | Default   | Remarks |
| -------- | --------- | --------- | ------- |
| $text    | string    |           | CSV text |
| $options | array     | `array()` | See below |
| $source  | string    | `'CSV'`   | Name shown in error messages (a file name) |

### Options

| Key      | Default   | Remarks |
| -------- | --------- | ------- |
| header   | `false`   | `true`: the first row holds the titles, and each row is keyed by them |
| columns  | `array()` | With `header`: `array('Title in the file' => 'key', ...)`. Rows are keyed by these keys, other columns are left out, a missing title is an error. Titles are compared trimmed |
| encoding | `'auto'`  | `'auto'` (UTF-8 when the text is valid UTF-8, otherwise Shift_JIS), `'utf-8'` or `'sjis'`. A BOM always means UTF-8 |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Tool\Csv;

$text = "タイトル,メモ,投稿日\r\nHello,,2026-11-15\r\n\r\n\"Two\r\nlines\",x,2026-11-16\r\n";

$rows = Csv::parse($text, array('header' => true, 'columns' => array('投稿日' => 'post_date', 'タイトル' => 'title')));
// array(
//     2 => array('post_date' => '2026-11-15', 'title' => 'Hello'),
//     4 => array('post_date' => '2026-11-16', 'title' => "Two\r\nlines"),
// )
```

Row 3 is empty and skipped, so the keys jump from 2 to 4.


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/csv/methods/parsefile ---


# Csv::parseFile

> Read a CSV file

## Description

```php
public static array Csv::parseFile( string $path [, array $options = array() ] )
```

Reads the file and returns its rows as [parse()](./parse.mdx) does; error messages start with the path.

Throws `\RuntimeException` when the file cannot be read or a row is broken.

## Arguments

| Name     | Data type | Default   | Remarks |
| -------- | --------- | --------- | ------- |
| $path    | string    |           | Path of the file |
| $options | array     | `array()` | `header`, `columns`, `encoding`: see [parse()](./parse.mdx#options) |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Tool\Csv;

$shops = Csv::parseFile(FEGG_SITE_DIR . '/config/shops.csv', array('header' => true));

foreach ($shops as $line => $shop) {
    // $shop['店名'], $shop['住所'] ...
}
```

For a file sent from a form, use [parseUpload()](./parseupload.mdx).


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/csv/methods/parseupload ---


# Csv::parseUpload

> Read a CSV file sent with a form

## Description

```php
public static array Csv::parseUpload( string $field [, array $options = array() ] )
```

Reads the file sent in the file input `$field` from PHP's temporary file and returns its rows as [parse()](./parse.mdx) does. Nothing is stored. Error messages start with the name of the sent file.

Only a file that PHP received as an upload in this request is read (`is_uploaded_file()`), with a name ending in `.csv` or `.txt`, of at most `max_size` bytes.

Throws `\RuntimeException` when:

- no file was sent, or more than one in the field;
- the file is larger than PHP accepts (`upload_max_filesize`) or than `max_size`;
- the upload failed;
- the name does not end in `.csv` or `.txt`;
- a row is broken or a title of `columns` is missing.

## Arguments

| Name     | Data type | Default   | Remarks |
| -------- | --------- | --------- | ------- |
| $field   | string    |           | Name of the file input |
| $options | array     | `array()` | `max_size` (bytes, default `5 * 1024 * 1024`: 5 MB), and `header`, `columns`, `encoding` of [parse()](./parse.mdx#options) |

## Example

```html title="Template"
<form action="{{ $site.prefix }}/import" method="post" enctype="multipart/form-data">
    {{ include '/cms/parts/csrf' }}
    <input type="file" name="csv" accept=".csv">
    <button type="submit">Import</button>
</form>
```

```php title="CMS controller"
use Fegg\Tool\Csv;

try {
    $rows = Csv::parseUpload('csv', array(
        'header'   => true,
        'columns'  => array('タイトル' => 'title', '投稿日' => 'post_date'),
        'max_size' => 1024 * 1024,
    ));
} catch (\RuntimeException $e) {
    $this->page['error'] = array($e->getMessage());
    $this->displayPage($this->page['prefix'] . '/import');
}
```

The whole import, with the check of every row: [Importing a file](../index.mdx#importing-a-file).


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/csv/methods/rowerrors ---


# Csv::rowErrors

> Check every row and collect the messages by row number

## Description

```php
public static array Csv::rowErrors( array $rows, callable $validate )
```

Calls `$validate` for each row and returns the messages of the rows that fail, keyed by row number: `array(3 => array('...', '...'), 7 => array('...'))`. Returns an empty array when every row is fine.

`$validate` takes a row and returns its messages as an array, or `false`, `null` or an empty array when the row is fine. The return value of `Validation::getErrorMessage()` fits directly.

## Arguments

| Name      | Data type | Default | Remarks |
| --------- | --------- | ------- | ------- |
| $rows     | array     |         | Rows from [parse()](./parse.mdx), [parseFile()](./parsefile.mdx) or [parseUpload()](./parseupload.mdx), keyed by row number |
| $validate | callable  |         | `function (array $row)`: returns the messages of the row |

## Example

```php title="CMS controller"
use Fegg\Tool\Csv;
use Fegg\Tool\Validation;

$errors = Csv::rowErrors($rows, function (array $row) {
    $validation = new Validation();
    $validation->required('title', $row['title'], array('@required', 'タイトル'));
    $validation->maxlength('title', $row['title'], 100, array('@maxlength', 'タイトル', 100));
    return $validation->getErrorMessage();
});

foreach ($errors as $line => $messages) {
    $this->page['error'][] = $line . '行目: ' . implode(' ', $messages);
}
```

Create a new `Validation` for each row, so the messages of one row do not carry over to the next.


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/csv/methods/field ---


# Csv::field

> One value as a CSV field

## Description

```php
public static string Csv::field( mixed $value [, bool $escapeFormulas = true ] )
```

Returns `$value` as [toString()](./tostring.mdx) writes it: converted to text, with a `'` before a formula, then quoted when needed (see [Writing](../index.mdx#writing)). Use it to write CSV of your own, a line at a time.

Throws `\InvalidArgumentException` when `$value` is an array.

## Arguments

| Name            | Data type | Default | Remarks |
| --------------- | --------- | ------- | ------- |
| $value          | mixed     |         | String, number, bool, `null` or date |
| $escapeFormulas | bool      | `true`  | `false`: no `'` before text starting with `=` `+` `-` `@` |

## Example

```php
use Fegg\Tool\Csv;

Csv::field('Hello');           // Hello
Csv::field('a,b');             // "a,b"
Csv::field('say "hi"');        // "say ""hi"""
Csv::field('=1+2');            // '=1+2
Csv::field('-5');              // -5 (numeric text is not escaped)
Csv::field(-5);                // -5
Csv::field(null);              // (empty)
Csv::field(true);              // 1
Csv::field(new \DateTime('2026-11-15 10:00'));   // 2026-11-15 10:00:00
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/database ---


import { CategoryDocCardList } from '@site/src/components/CategoryDocCardList';

# Introduction

> Run SQL on the database connection

`Fegg\Database\Pdo` builds and runs SQL on top of PHP's PDO. [Fegg\Model](../model/index.mdx) uses it for every query.

:::note

Usually you don't need this class: use [Fegg\Model](../model/index.mdx), which also checks names, applies the visible flag and pages results. Use `Pdo` for raw SQL the model cannot express.

:::

## How to use

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Database\Pdo;

$db = new Pdo();

// Raw SQL with ? placeholders
$rows = $db->setQuery(
    'SELECT category, count(*) AS n FROM cms_news WHERE valid = 1 GROUP BY category HAVING n > ?',
    array(1)
)->execute()->all();

// Or build the query
$rows = $db->setWhere('category = ?', 2)->setOrder('post_date DESC')->select('cms_news')->execute()->all();
```

A query is built with the `set*` methods and a build method ([select](./methods/select.mdx), [count](./methods/count.mdx), [insert](./methods/insert.mdx), [update](./methods/update.mdx), [delete](./methods/delete.mdx) ...), or written with [setQuery](./methods/setquery.mdx). [execute](./methods/execute.mdx) runs it, then [all](./methods/all.mdx), [one](./methods/one.mdx), [getAffectedRow](./methods/getaffectedrow.mdx) or [getLastIndexId](./methods/getlastindexid.mdx) read the result. After `execute()` the built query is forgotten, so each query starts clean.

### Automatic queries

Queries built with the build methods get the automatic parts of `database_regular_use_query.php` (see [Configuration](./configuration.mdx)): `valid = 1` on SELECT and count, `created` / `modified` / `valid` on INSERT and `modified` on UPDATE. Queries written with `setQuery()` get nothing added.

## Values and placeholders

- Values are always bound to `?` placeholders. Integers and booleans are bound as integers, `null` as NULL, everything else as strings (so `count(*) > ?` works on SQLite).
- In [setWhere](./methods/setwhere.mdx), [setHaving](./methods/sethaving.mdx) and [setOn](./methods/seton.mdx), an array bound to one placeholder expands it: `id = ?` and `id IN (?)` become `id IN (?, ?, ...)`, `<>` / `!=` / `NOT IN` become `NOT IN`, `LIKE ?` becomes `(... LIKE ? OR ... LIKE ?)` and `NOT LIKE ?` becomes `(... NOT LIKE ? AND ...)`. An empty array matches no row (`=`, `IN`, `LIKE`) or every row (`<>`, `NOT IN`, `NOT LIKE`). An array on any other operator (`<`, `BETWEEN` ...) throws `InvalidArgumentException`.
- A `?` inside a quoted string literal (`'?'`) is not a placeholder.
- Table, column and ORDER BY text given to the `set*` methods is written into the SQL as it is: never put user input in it.

## Errors

A failed connection or query throws `Fegg\Database\QueryException` (a `RuntimeException`). Its message ends with the SQL with its values filled in, for example `no such column: nope (SQL: SELECT nope FROM cms_news WHERE title = 'it''s')`. `getSql()` returns the SQL with its `?` placeholders and `getParameters()` the values. Uncaught, it becomes an HTTP 500 and is written to the PHP error log and the [application log](../../basic/logging.mdx).

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Database\QueryException;

try {
    $db->setQuery($sql, $params)->execute();
} catch (QueryException $e) {
    // e.g. a duplicate key
    error_log($e->getMessage());
}
```

## Connections and transactions

- Connections are opened once per DSN and user name and shared by every `Pdo` instance and every model.
- SELECT and PRAGMA queries run on a slave, other queries on the master (see [Configuration](./configuration.mdx)). [masterServer](./methods/masterserver.mdx) and [slaveServer](./methods/slaveserver.mdx) choose the server for the next query.
- [beginTransaction](./methods/begintransaction.mdx) opens a transaction on the master. Until [commit](./methods/commit.mdx) or [rollBack](./methods/rollback.mdx), every query of every instance and every model runs inside it. [Model::transaction](../model/methods/transaction.mdx) does this for you.

## Methods

<CategoryDocCardList label="Methods" />


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/database/configuration ---


# Configuration

### Database connect setting

The connection is set in **htdocs/\_fegg/site/config/database\_config.php**. The skeleton's file reads these values with `feggEnv()` (an environment variable, then `site/config/env.php`, see [Settings](../../first/config/environments.mdx)), so the file itself is committed and the same on every server.

| Name                | Default                     | Remarks |
| ------------------- | --------------------------- | ------- |
| `FEGG_DB_DSN`       | `sqlite:` + `FEGG_DB_PATH`  | PDO DSN |
| `FEGG_DB_USER`      | `''`                        | User name (MySQL) |
| `FEGG_DB_PASSWORD`  | `''`                        | Password (MySQL) |
| `FEGG_DB_SLAVE_DSN` | `FEGG_DB_DSN`               | Read-only replica |
| `FEGG_DB_PATH`      | `site/data/db/db.db`        | SQLite file. As an environment variable without `FEGG_DB_DSN`, it wins over `FEGG_DB_DSN` in `env.php` (the test suite uses it) |

The file fills two entries. `dsn`, `username` and `password` are passed to PHP's `PDO`:

```php title="site/config/database_config.php (shape)"
$database_config['master'] = array('dsn' => ..., 'username' => ..., 'password' => ...);
$database_config['slave']  = array(
    array('dsn' => ..., 'username' => ..., 'password' => ...), // more than one server allowed
);
```

- `master` is used for INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE, schema changes and transactions. SELECT and PRAGMA queries use one of the `slave` entries, picked at random. With a single database, both use the same DSN.
- `new \Fegg\Database\Pdo('name')` reads `name/database_config.php` and `name/database_regular_use_query.php` instead, for a second database.

### MySQL

SQLite is the default. For MySQL (5.7 / 8.x) or MariaDB, set the connection in `site/config/env.php` (or as environment variables):

```php title="site/config/env.php"
'FEGG_DB_DSN'      => 'mysql:host=127.0.0.1;port=3306;dbname=example;charset=utf8mb4',
'FEGG_DB_USER'     => 'example',
'FEGG_DB_PASSWORD' => '...',
```

- Always add **`charset=utf8mb4`** to the DSN, or emoji and other 4-byte characters are lost.
- The connection runs with `sql_mode = ANSI_QUOTES`: Fegg quotes names with `"`, so in raw SQL write string literals with **single quotes** (`WHERE title = 'foo'`, not `"foo"`, which is a column name). Queries are buffered.
- `./fegg-cli migration`, `migration:db2schema` and `/cms/migrate/` work on MySQL as on SQLite. Tables are created as InnoDB with `utf8mb4_unicode_ci`, and changed tables are altered in place (`ADD` / `MODIFY` / `DROP COLUMN`).

The YAML types are mapped like this (a size written in the YAML, such as `VARCHAR(100)`, is used as written):

| YAML type  | MySQL type     |
| :--------- | :------------- |
| `INTEGER`  | `INT`          |
| `CHAR`     | `VARCHAR(255)` |
| `TEXT`     | `MEDIUMTEXT`   |
| `BOOLEAN`  | `TINYINT(1)`   |
| `DATETIME` | `DATETIME`     |
| `BLOB`     | `LONGBLOB`     |
| `REAL`     | `DOUBLE`       |

The YAML type is kept in the column comment (`fegg:CHAR`), so `migration:db2schema` writes the same YAML back. Indexes on `TEXT` columns use the first 191 characters.

`fegg-cli migration` drops the tables and columns that are not in the YAML files (see [migration](../../first/cli.mdx#migration)): give the site a database of its own, never one shared with another application.

The skeleton's MySQL tests run with `composer test:mysql` against the server set in `FEGG_TEST_MYSQL_DSN`, `FEGG_TEST_MYSQL_USER` and `FEGG_TEST_MYSQL_PASSWORD`. Each test creates and drops its own database.

### Automatic append query configuration

The queries added automatically to queries built by `Fegg\Model` and the build methods of `Fegg\Database\Pdo` are set in **htdocs/\_fegg/core/config/database_regular_use_query.php**. To change them, copy the file to `site/config/` (see [Overriding the core](../../first/cont.mdx#overriding-the-core)); the whole file is replaced.

`CURRENT_TABLE` is replaced with the table name, and `now()` with the current date and time. An item that the query already sets (for example `valid` given to `insertRow()`) is not added again.

```php title="core/config/database_regular_use_query.php"
<?php
defined('FEGG_CORE_DIR') || exit;

/**
 * Automatically appended queries
 *
 * Queries appended automatically when the "select", "insert", "update" and "delete" methods of the DB class run
 * (to be extended as needed).
 */
$database_regular_use_query = array(
    // Queries appended regardless of the table
    'regular_use' => array(
        'select' => array(
            'where' => 'CURRENT_TABLE.valid = 1',
            'order' => '',
        ),
        'count' => array(
            'where' => 'CURRENT_TABLE.valid = 1',
            'order' => '',
        ),
        'insert' => array(
            'item' => 'modified = now(), created = now(), valid = 1',
        ),
        'update' => array(
            'item' => "modified = now()",
            'where' => '',
        ),
    ),

    /*
    // Queries appended per table
    'table' => array(
        'table_name' => array(
            'select' => array(
                'where' => 'show_flag = 0',
                'order' => '',
            ),
            'insert' => array(
                'item' => 'update_time = now(), create_datetime = now()',
            ),
            'update' => array(
                'item' => 'update_time = now()',
                'where' => '',
            ),
        ),
    ),
    */
);
```

These queries are why the model needs its [required columns](../model/index.mdx#required-columns). To skip these queries, see `unsetRegularUseQuery()`, `unsetRegularUseQueryForTable()` and `disabledRegularUseQuery()` ([Model](../model/methods/unsetregularusequery.mdx), [Pdo](./methods/unsetregularusequery.mdx)).


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/database/methods/construct ---


# Pdo::\_\_construct

> Pdo class constructor

## Description

```php
public Pdo::__construct( [ string $env = '' ] )
```

Loads `database_config.php` and `database_regular_use_query.php` (see [Configuration](../configuration.mdx)). Nothing is connected until the first query; connections are shared by every instance.

## Arguments

| Name | Data type | Default | Remarks                                                                                              |
| ---- | --------- | ------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| $env | string    | ''      | Config sub directory: `'name'` reads `name/database_config.php` and `name/database_regular_use_query.php` |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Database\Pdo;

$db = new Pdo();
$rows = $db->setQuery('SELECT * FROM cms_news WHERE valid = 1 AND category = ?', array(2))->execute()->all();
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/database/methods/select ---


# Pdo::select

> Set select query

## Description

```php
public self Pdo::select( string $table )
```

Builds a SELECT on `$table` from what was set with [setItem](./setitem.mdx) (columns, default `*`), [setJoin](./setjoin.mdx) / [setOn](./seton.mdx), [setWhere](./setwhere.mdx), [setGroup](./setgroup.mdx), [setHaving](./sethaving.mdx), [setOrder](./setorder.mdx) and [setLimit](./setlimit.mdx). `CURRENT_TABLE.valid = 1` is added (see [Configuration](../configuration.mdx)). Run it with [execute](./execute.mdx).

## Arguments

| Name   | Data type | Default | Remarks           |
| ------ | --------- | ------- | ----------------- |
| $table | string    |         | Target table name |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
$db = new \Fegg\Database\Pdo();

// Select * From "cms_news" Where category = ? And "cms_news".valid = 1 Order By post_date DESC Limit 0, 10
$rows = $db->setWhere('category = ?', 2)
    ->setOrder('post_date DESC')
    ->setLimit(10)
    ->select('cms_news')
    ->execute()
    ->all();
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/database/methods/count ---


# Pdo::count

> Set select count query

## Description

```php
public self Pdo::count( string $table )
```

Builds `Select Count(*) as number_of_records From (...) AS "count_temp"` around the SELECT that [select](./select.mdx) would build, so conditions, joins and groups are counted correctly. Read the result with `one()['number_of_records']`.

## Arguments

| Name   | Data type | Default | Remarks           |
| ------ | --------- | ------- | ----------------- |
| $table | string    |         | Target table name |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
$db = new \Fegg\Database\Pdo();

$record = $db->setWhere('category = ?', 2)->count('cms_news')->execute()->one();
$count = (int) $record['number_of_records'];
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/database/methods/insert ---


# Pdo::insert

> Set insert query

## Description

```php
public self Pdo::insert( string $table )
```

Builds an INSERT on `$table` from the values set with [setValues](./setvalues.mdx) (or [setItem](./setitem.mdx)). `created`, `modified` and `valid` are added unless given (see [Configuration](../configuration.mdx)). Run it with [execute](./execute.mdx) and read the new ID with [getLastIndexId](./getlastindexid.mdx).

## Arguments

| Name   | Data type | Default | Remarks           |
| ------ | --------- | ------- | ----------------- |
| $table | string    |         | Target table name |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
$db = new \Fegg\Database\Pdo();

// Insert Into "cms_news" ("title", "category", modified, created, valid) Values (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
$db->setValues(array('title' => 'Sample', 'category' => 2))->insert('cms_news')->execute();
$id = $db->getLastIndexId();
// -> '24'
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/database/methods/update ---


# Pdo::update

> Set update query

## Description

```php
public self Pdo::update( string $table )
```

Builds an UPDATE on `$table` from the values set with [setValues](./setvalues.mdx) (or [setItem](./setitem.mdx)) and [setIncrement](./setincrement.mdx), and the condition set with [setWhere](./setwhere.mdx). `modified` is added unless given. Run it with [execute](./execute.mdx) and read the number of updated rows with [getAffectedRow](./getaffectedrow.mdx).

Unlike `Model::updateRowsWhere()`, `Pdo` does not refuse an UPDATE without a condition: without `setWhere()` every row is updated.

## Arguments

| Name   | Data type | Default | Remarks           |
| ------ | --------- | ------- | ----------------- |
| $table | string    |         | Target table name |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
$db = new \Fegg\Database\Pdo();

// Update "cms_news" Set "title"= ?, modified = ? Where news_id IN (?, ?)
$db->setValues(array('title' => 'New title'))
    ->setWhere('news_id IN (?)', array(1, 2))
    ->update('cms_news')
    ->execute();
$updated = $db->getAffectedRow();
// -> 2
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/database/methods/delete ---


# Pdo::delete

> Set delete query

## Description

```php
public self Pdo::delete( [ string $table = '' ] )
```

Builds a `DELETE` on `$table` with the condition set with [setWhere](./setwhere.mdx). This removes the rows; for a soft delete (`valid = 0`) use `Model::delete()`.

`Pdo` does not refuse a DELETE without a condition: without `setWhere()` every row is removed.

## Arguments

| Name   | Data type | Default | Remarks           |
| ------ | --------- | ------- | ----------------- |
| $table | string    | ''      | Target table name |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
$db = new \Fegg\Database\Pdo();

// Delete From "cms_news" Where news_id = ?
$db->setWhere('news_id = ?', 3)->delete('cms_news')->execute();
$deleted = $db->getAffectedRow();
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/database/methods/truncate ---


# Pdo::truncate

> Set truncate query

## Description

```php
public self Pdo::truncate( string $table )
```

Builds `Truncate "table"`, which removes every row. MySQL only: SQLite has no TRUNCATE (use [delete](./delete.mdx) without a condition there).

## Arguments

| Name   | Data type | Default | Remarks           |
| ------ | --------- | ------- | ----------------- |
| $table | string    |         | Target table name |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
$db = new \Fegg\Database\Pdo();

// Truncate "sample_log"
$db->truncate('sample_log')->execute();
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/database/methods/createtable ---


# Pdo::createTable

> Set create table query

## Description

```php
public self Pdo::createTable( string $table, array $field )
```

Builds a `Create Table`. Tables are normally defined in `site/config/database/*.yaml` and created with `./fegg-cli migration`.

## Arguments

| Name   | Data type | Default | Remarks                             |
| ------ | --------- | ------- | ----------------------------------- |
| $table | string    |         | Table name                          |
| $field | array     |         | List of column definitions (below)  |

### Column definition

Every key must be present.

| Key          | Data type | Remarks                                          |
| ------------ | --------- | ------------------------------------------------ |
| `name`       | string    | Column name                                      |
| `type`       | string    | Column type                                      |
| `notnull`    | boolean   | Add NOT NULL                                     |
| `dflt_value` | mixed     | Default value (`null`, `''` or `0`: no default)  |
| `pk`         | boolean   | Add PRIMARY KEY                                  |
| `ai`         | boolean   | Add AUTOINCREMENT                                |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
$db = new \Fegg\Database\Pdo();

// Create Table "sample" (id INTEGER NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, title TEXT DEFAULT 'none')
$db->createTable('sample', array(
    array('name' => 'id',    'type' => 'INTEGER', 'notnull' => true,  'dflt_value' => null,   'pk' => true,  'ai' => true),
    array('name' => 'title', 'type' => 'TEXT',    'notnull' => false, 'dflt_value' => 'none', 'pk' => false, 'ai' => false),
))->execute();
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/database/methods/createindex ---


# Pdo::createIndex

> Set create index query

## Description

```php
public self Pdo::createIndex( string $table, array $field )
```

Builds `Create [UNIQUE] Index "name" ON "table" (columns)`.

## Arguments

| Name   | Data type | Default | Remarks                    |
| ------ | --------- | ------- | -------------------------- |
| $table | string    |         | Table name                 |
| $field | array     |         | Index definition (below)   |

### Index definition

| Key      | Data type | Remarks                              |
| -------- | --------- | ------------------------------------ |
| `name`   | string    | Index name                           |
| `unique` | boolean   | Create a UNIQUE index                |
| `target` | string    | Columns, separated by commas         |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
$db = new \Fegg\Database\Pdo();

// Create UNIQUE Index "sample_title" ON "sample" (title)
$db->createIndex('sample', array('name' => 'sample_title', 'unique' => true, 'target' => 'title'))->execute();
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/database/methods/alteraddcolumn ---


# Pdo::alterAddColumn

> Set create alter add column query

## Description

```php
public self Pdo::alterAddColumn( string $table, array $field )
```

Builds `Alter Table "table" ADD COLUMN ...`.

## Arguments

| Name   | Data type | Default | Remarks                                                                  |
| ------ | --------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| $table | string    |         | Table name                                                               |
| $field | array     |         | Column definition, with every key described in [createTable](./createtable.mdx) |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
$db = new \Fegg\Database\Pdo();

// Alter Table "sample" ADD COLUMN note TEXT
$db->alterAddColumn('sample', array(
    'name'       => 'note',
    'type'       => 'TEXT',
    'notnull'    => false,
    'dflt_value' => null,
    'pk'         => false,
    'ai'         => false,
))->execute();
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/database/methods/alterrenameto ---


# Pdo::alterRenameTo

> Set create alter rename to query

## Description

```php
public self Pdo::alterRenameTo( string $table, string $rename )
```

Builds `Alter Table "table" RENAME TO "rename"`.

## Arguments

| Name    | Data type | Default | Remarks         |
| ------- | --------- | ------- | --------------- |
| $table  | string    |         | Table name      |
| $rename | string    |         | New table name  |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
$db = new \Fegg\Database\Pdo();

// Alter Table "sample" RENAME TO "sample_old"
$db->alterRenameTo('sample', 'sample_old')->execute();
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/database/methods/droptable ---

```php
public self Pdo::dropTable( string $table )
```

Builds `Drop Table "table"`.

## Arguments

| Name   | Data type | Default | Remarks    |
| ------ | --------- | ------- | ---------- |
| $table | string    |         | Table name |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
$db = new \Fegg\Database\Pdo();

// Drop Table "sample_old"
$db->dropTable('sample_old')->execute();
```
