# Model::pragma

> Execute Pragma

## Description

```php
public array Model::pragma( string $mode )
```

Runs an SQLite `PRAGMA` on the model's table and returns its rows. SQLite only.

## Arguments

| Name  | Data type | Default | Remarks     |
| ----- | --------- | ------- | ----------- |
| $mode | string    |         | Pragma mode |

### Pragma mode

| Mode         | Query                                                 |
| ------------ | ----------------------------------------------------- |
| `table`      | `PRAGMA table_info("table")` (also any unknown mode)  |
| `index_list` | `PRAGMA index_list("table")`                          |
| `index`      | `PRAGMA index_info("table")`                          |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
$model = new Model(true, array(
    'table' => 'cms_news',
));

// PRAGMA table_info("cms_news")
$columns = $model->pragma('table');

print_r($columns[0]);
```

```php title="Output"
Array
(
    [cid] => 0
    [name] => news_id
    [type] => INTEGER
    [notnull] => 1
    [dflt_value] =>
    [pk] => 1
)
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/model/methods/reset ---


# Model::reset

> Reset Model

## Description

```php
public self Model::reset()
```

Forgets the conditions set with the chain methods: where, having, order, join, group, select, listMax, current and limit. The model does this by itself after every query, failed ones included, so call it only to drop a chain you built but did not run. The table, primary key, visible flag, bind types and the pager are kept.

## Example

```php title="Controller"
$model = new Model(true, array(
    'table' => 'cms_news',
    'id'    => 'news_id',
));

$model->where('category = ?', 1)->order('post_date', 'desc');

// Changed our mind: start again
$model->reset();

// Select * From "cms_news" Where 1 = 1 And "cms_news".valid = 1
$records = $model->all();
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/model/methods/debug ---


# Model::debug

> Print the last executed query

## Description

```php
public void Model::debug()
```

Prints (`echo`) the last query of this model with its values filled in. The values are quoted for reading only: never run the result. To see a SELECT without running it, use [toSql](./tosql.mdx).

## Example

```php title="Controller"
$model = new Model(false, array(
    'table'   => 'cms_news',
    'id'      => 'news_id',
    'visible' => 'visible',
));

$model->where('category = ?', 1)
    ->listMax(5)
    ->current(2)
    ->find();

$model->debug();
// -> Select * From "cms_news" Where category = 1 AND cms_news.visible = '1' And "cms_news".valid = 1 Limit 5, 5
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/model/methods/unsetregularusequery ---


# Model::unsetRegularUseQuery

> Skip the automatic queries for the next query

## Description

```php
public self Model::unsetRegularUseQuery()
```

For the next query only, skips the queries added for every table (`regular_use` in `database_regular_use_query.php`, see [Configuration](../../database/configuration.mdx)): the `valid = 1` condition of reads, `created` / `modified` / `valid` on insert and `modified` on update. [disabledRegularUseQuery](./disabledregularusequery.mdx) skips them for good.

## Example

```php title="Controller"
$model = new Model(true, array(
    'table' => 'cms_news',
    'id'    => 'news_id',
));

// Include the rows deleted with delete() (valid = 0)
$records = $model->unsetRegularUseQuery()->all();

// Count a view without changing "modified" (the table has a view_count column)
$model->unsetRegularUseQuery()->increment('view_count', 1);
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/model/methods/unsetregularusequeryfortable ---


# Model::unsetRegularUseQueryForTable

> Skip the automatic queries of the table for the next query

## Description

```php
public self Model::unsetRegularUseQueryForTable()
```

For the next query only, skips the queries configured for this table (the `table` section of `database_regular_use_query.php`, see [Configuration](../../database/configuration.mdx)). The queries added for every table still apply; see [unsetRegularUseQuery](./unsetregularusequery.mdx).

## Example

```php title="Controller"
$model = new Model(true, array(
    'table' => 'cms_news',
    'id'    => 'news_id',
));

$records = $model->unsetRegularUseQueryForTable()->all();
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/model/methods/disabledregularusequery ---


# Model::disabledRegularUseQuery

> Skip the automatic queries for good

## Description

```php
public self Model::disabledRegularUseQuery()
```

Skips every automatic query (for every table and for this table, see [Configuration](../../database/configuration.mdx)) for every later query of this model instance: reads also return rows with `valid = 0`, and inserts and updates no longer fill `created`, `modified` and `valid`. To skip them for one query only, use [unsetRegularUseQuery](./unsetregularusequery.mdx).

## Example

```php title="Controller"
// A table without valid / created / modified columns
$model = new Model(true, array(
    'table' => 'sample_log',
    'id'    => 'log_id',
));
$model->disabledRegularUseQuery();

$records = $model->all();
$model->insertRow(array('message' => 'Hello'));
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/rate-limit ---


import { CategoryDocCardList } from '@site/src/components/CategoryDocCardList';

# Introduction

> Limiting how often one visitor can do something

`Fegg\Tool\RateLimit` allows at most `$max` attempts in `$window` seconds for one key: an IP address, a login ID. Use it for forms that send mail, logins, and anything a bot could repeat. The attempts are counted in the core table `rate_limit`.

## How to use

Import `Fegg\Tool\RateLimit`, create it with a bucket name, the attempts allowed and the window, and call [attempt()](./methods/attempt.mdx). It returns `true` when the attempt is **refused**.

```php title="Controller"
<?php
namespace Site\Controller;

defined('FEGG_CORE_DIR') || exit;

use Fegg\Tool\RateLimit;

class Entry extends Controller
{
    public function post()
    {
        $this->requirePost();

        // 5 entries an hour from one address
        $limit = new RateLimit('entry', 5, 3600);
        if ($limit->attempt((string) $_SERVER['REMOTE_ADDR'])) {
            $this->redirect('/entry/error.html');
        }

        // ... check and save the entry
    }
}
```

The bucket names the limit: the same key is counted apart in each bucket.

## How attempts are counted

- An attempt counts for `$window` seconds from the moment it was made (a sliding window, not fixed hours).
- [attempt()](./methods/attempt.mdx) stores the attempt **first**, then counts. Requests sent in parallel cannot all pass the check before any of them is stored.
- A refused attempt removes its own row, so a client that keeps trying does not extend its lock: it can try again once its oldest counted attempt is older than the window.
- The key itself is not stored: each row holds the bucket and a sha256 hash of the bucket and the key, so the table holds no addresses or login IDs.
- Now and then (on about one allowed attempt in 20), rows older than one day, or than the window when it is longer, are removed.

## The table

`rate_limit` is a core table (`core/config/database/rate_limit.yaml`), created by `fegg-cli migration` (see [fegg-cli](../../first/cli.mdx#migration)).

**Without the table nothing is limited**: every attempt is allowed, so the page keeps working, and one line per request goes to the PHP error log:

```text
Fegg: rate limiting is off (...). Run `fegg-cli migration` to create the table rate_limit.
```

Run `fegg-cli migration` after deploying a project that uses `RateLimit` for the first time.

## In the skeleton

| Where | Bucket | Key | Limit |
| :---- | :----- | :-- | :---- |
| CMS login (`core/src/Controller/Cms/Login.php`) | `cms-login` | Login ID + address | 5 in 15 minutes |
| CMS login | `cms-login-address` | Address | 20 in 15 minutes |
| Contact form (`site/src/Controller/Contact.php`) | `contact:<template>` | Address | `rate_limit_per_hour` an hour |

The CMS login takes back the attempt of the address when the per-login limit refuses ([undo()](./methods/undo.mdx)), and after a successful login forgets the failures of that login ([clear()](./methods/clear.mdx)) and takes back the attempt of the address. A locked login shows a message on the login page.

The contact form counts sent mails, checked just before sending. Its limit is `$_RATE_LIMIT_PER_HOUR` in `site/config/contact/contact_config.php` (5 by default; `0` turns it off):

```php title="htdocs/_fegg/site/config/contact/contact_config.php"
// Mails one visitor (IP address) can send per hour (Fegg\Tool\RateLimit); 0 = no limit
$_RATE_LIMIT_PER_HOUR = 5;
```

:::note

The skeleton uses `$_SERVER['REMOTE_ADDR']` as the address. Behind a reverse proxy or a CDN that does not pass the visitor's address to PHP there, every visitor has the proxy's address and shares one limit.

:::

## Errors

- A bucket name that is empty or longer than 64 characters, or `$max` / `$window` below 1, throws `\InvalidArgumentException`.
- A missing table does not throw (see [The table](#the-table)).

## Methods

<CategoryDocCardList label="Methods" />


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/rate-limit/methods/construct ---


# RateLimit::__construct

> Create a limit

## Description

```php
public RateLimit::__construct( string $bucket, int $max, int $window )
```

Creates a limit of `$max` attempts in `$window` seconds. Keys are counted apart in each bucket.

Throws `\InvalidArgumentException` when `$bucket` is empty or longer than 64 characters, or `$max` or `$window` is below 1.

## Arguments

| Name    | Data type | Default | Remarks |
| ------- | --------- | ------- | ------- |
| $bucket | string    |         | Name of the limit (1 to 64 characters): `'contact'`, `'cms-login'` |
| $max    | int       |         | Attempts allowed in the window |
| $window | int       |         | Seconds |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Tool\RateLimit;

$perHour = new RateLimit('entry', 5, 3600);        // 5 an hour
$perDay  = new RateLimit('entry-day', 20, 86400);  // and 20 a day
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/rate-limit/methods/attempt ---


# RateLimit::attempt

> Record an attempt and check the limit

## Description

```php
public bool RateLimit::attempt( string $key )
```

Stores an attempt for `$key`, then counts the attempts of the window including it. Returns `true` when there are more than `$max`: the attempt is **refused** and its row removed again. Returns `false` when it is allowed; the attempt then counts until the window has passed, unless [undo()](./undo.mdx) takes it back.

Without the table `rate_limit` it always returns `false` (see [The table](../index.mdx#the-table)).

## Arguments

| Name | Data type | Default | Remarks |
| ---- | --------- | ------- | ------- |
| $key | string    |         | Who is counted: an IP address, a login ID ... |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Log;
use Fegg\Tool\RateLimit;

$ip = isset($_SERVER['REMOTE_ADDR']) ? (string) $_SERVER['REMOTE_ADDR'] : '';
if ((new RateLimit('contact', 5, 3600))->attempt($ip)) {
    Log::warning('Contact form: more than 5 mails an hour from {ip}', array('ip' => $ip));
    $this->redirect('/contact/error.html');
}
```

Call it where the work is done (just before sending the mail, when checking the password), not when the form is shown.


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/rate-limit/methods/islimited ---


# RateLimit::isLimited

> Check whether the next attempt would be refused

## Description

```php
public bool RateLimit::isLimited( string $key )
```

Returns `true` when `$key` already has `$max` attempts in the window. Nothing is recorded.

Use it to show a message before the visitor fills in a form. To enforce the limit, use [attempt()](./attempt.mdx): two requests can both see `isLimited()` return `false`.

## Arguments

| Name | Data type | Default | Remarks |
| ---- | --------- | ------- | ------- |
| $key | string    |         | Who is counted |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Tool\RateLimit;

$this->page['limited'] = (new RateLimit('contact', 5, 3600))->isLimited((string) $_SERVER['REMOTE_ADDR']);
$this->displayPage('contact/index');
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/rate-limit/methods/remaining ---


# RateLimit::remaining

> Attempts left in the window

## Description

```php
public int RateLimit::remaining( string $key )
```

Returns how many attempts `$key` has left in the current window (`0` when it is limited). Nothing is recorded. Without the table `rate_limit` it returns `$max`.

## Arguments

| Name | Data type | Default | Remarks |
| ---- | --------- | ------- | ------- |
| $key | string    |         | Who is counted |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Tool\RateLimit;

$limit = new RateLimit('download', 10, 86400);
$this->page['downloads_left'] = $limit->remaining((string) $this->getSession('member_id'));
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/rate-limit/methods/undo ---


# RateLimit::undo

> Take back the last allowed attempt

## Description

```php
public void RateLimit::undo()
```

Removes the attempt stored by the last allowed [attempt()](./attempt.mdx) of this object, when it turned out not to count. Nothing happens when the last attempt was refused or `undo()` was already called.

## Example

The CMS login checks two limits. When the second refuses, the attempt of the first is taken back, and a successful login does not count against the address:

```php title="htdocs/_fegg/core/src/Controller/Cms/Login.php"
$perAddress = new RateLimit('cms-login-address', 20, 900);
$perLogin   = new RateLimit('cms-login', 5, 900);
if ($perAddress->attempt($ip)) {
    $this->redirect($this->page['prefix'] . 'login/index/LOCKED');
}
if ($perLogin->attempt($loginKey)) {
    $perAddress->undo();
    $this->redirect($this->page['prefix'] . 'login/index/LOCKED');
}

// ... password checked
$perLogin->clear($loginKey);
$perAddress->undo();
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/rate-limit/methods/clear ---


# RateLimit::clear

> Forget the attempts of a key

## Description

```php
public void RateLimit::clear( string $key )
```

Removes every attempt of `$key` in this bucket, for example after a successful login, so earlier failures do not count any more.

## Arguments

| Name | Data type | Default | Remarks |
| ---- | --------- | ------- | ------- |
| $key | string    |         | Who is counted |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Tool\RateLimit;

$limit = new RateLimit('member-login', 5, 900);
if ($limit->attempt($loginId)) {
    $this->redirect('/member/login/?locked=1');
}

if ($this->_checkPassword($loginId, $password)) {
    $limit->clear($loginId);
    // ... log in
}
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/rss ---


import { CategoryDocCardList } from '@site/src/components/CategoryDocCardList';

# Introduction

> Reading RSS / Atom feeds

`Fegg\Tool\RSS` loads an RSS 1.0 (RDF), RSS 2.0 or Atom feed and returns its posts as arrays. A feed URL is fetched with [Fegg\Http\Client](../http-client/index.mdx) (10 second timeout by default); a local file can be read as well.

## How to use

Import `Fegg\Tool\RSS` and create it with `new`, passing the feed URL. The constructor throws a `\RuntimeException` when the feed cannot be fetched or read, or is not RSS or Atom, so catch it when the feed may be unavailable.

```php title="Controller"
<?php
namespace Site\Controller;

defined('FEGG_CORE_DIR') || exit;

use Fegg\Tool\RSS;

class Index extends Controller
{
    public function index()
    {
        try {
            $rss = new RSS('https://example.com/feed');
            $this->page['posts'] = $rss->fetchAll(10);
        } catch (\RuntimeException $e) {
            $this->page['posts'] = array();
        }

        $this->displayPage('index');
    }
}
```

## Entry data

Each entry returned by [RSS::fetch](./methods/fetch.mdx) and [RSS::fetchAll](./methods/fetchall.mdx) is an array with these keys. A field the feed does not have is `''` (an empty array for `category` and `tag`).

| Key         | Data type     | Remarks                                                         |
| ----------- | ------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------- |
| title       | string        | Title                                                           |
| pubDate     | string        | Date as written in the feed (RSS 2.0: `pubDate`, or `dc:date`; RSS 1.0: `dc:date`; Atom: `published`, or `updated`) |
| datetime    | DateTime/null | `pubDate` as a `DateTime` object, `null` when there is no date or it cannot be read |
| link        | string        | Link URL (Atom: the `href` of `<link rel="alternate">`)         |
| description | string        | Description (RSS: HTML entities decoded; Atom: `summary`)       |
| content     | string        | `content:encoded` (HTML entities decoded); Atom: `content`      |
| author      | string        | `dc:creator` (RSS 2.0: or `author`; Atom: `author` > `name`)    |
| category    | array         | Category names (RSS 2.0: `category`; RSS 1.0: `dc:subject`; Atom: the `term` of `category`) |
| tag         | array         | Always an empty array (not implemented)                         |

## Methods

<CategoryDocCardList label="Methods" />


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/rss/methods/construct ---


# RSS::\_\_construct

> Load RSS feed

## Description

```php
public RSS::__construct( string $url, [ array $options = array() ] )
```

Loads and parses an RSS 1.0, RSS 2.0 or Atom feed. The entries can then be read with [RSS::fetch](./fetch.mdx) or [RSS::fetchAll](./fetchall.mdx).

An `http://` or `https://` URL is fetched with [Fegg\Http\Client](../../http-client/index.mdx); any other value is read as a local file.

Throws a `\RuntimeException` when:

- the URL cannot be fetched (connection error, timeout, or an HTTP status other than 200);
- the file does not exist or cannot be read;
- the document is not XML, or is not an RSS or Atom feed.

## Arguments

| Name     | Data type | Default | Remarks                          |
| -------- | --------- | ------- | -------------------------------- |
| $url     | string    |         | Feed URL, or the path of a file  |
| $options | array     | array() | Options (see below)              |

| Key     | Data type | Default | Remarks                                                         |
| ------- | --------- | ------- | --------------------------------------------------------------- |
| skip_pr | boolean   | false   | `true`: leave out the posts whose title contains `PR:` (advertisements of some blog services) |
| timeout | int       | 10      | Seconds for the whole request (the connection: 3 seconds)       |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Tool\RSS;

try {
    $rss = new RSS('https://example.com/feed', array('skip_pr' => true, 'timeout' => 5));
} catch (\RuntimeException $e) {
    // cannot be fetched, or not a feed: $e->getMessage()
}

// A local file
$rss = new RSS(FEGG_SITE_DIR . '/data/feed.xml');
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/rss/methods/fetch ---


# RSS::fetch

> Fetch the next entry of RSS feed

## Description

```php
public mixed RSS::fetch()
```

Returns the next entry as an array (see [Entry data](../index.mdx#entry-data)), or `false` when there are no more entries.

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Tool\RSS;

$rss = new RSS('https://example.com/feed');

while ($post = $rss->fetch()) {
    // $post['title'], $post['link'], $post['datetime'] ...
}
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/rss/methods/fetchall ---


# RSS::fetchAll

> Fetch all entries or specified number of entries of RSS feed

## Description

```php
public array RSS::fetchAll( [ int $max = 0 ] )
```

Returns the remaining entries (see [Entry data](../index.mdx#entry-data)) as an array.

## Arguments

| Name | Data type | Default | Remarks                                         |
| ---- | --------- | ------- | ----------------------------------------------- |
| $max | int       | 0       | Maximum number of entries / `0`: all the entries |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Tool\RSS;

$rss = new RSS('https://example.com/feed');
$posts = $rss->fetchAll(5);

foreach ($posts as $post) {
    // $post['title'], $post['link'], $post['datetime'] ...
}
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/sitemap ---


import { CategoryDocCardList } from '@site/src/components/CategoryDocCardList';

# Introduction

> Writing sitemap.xml

`Fegg\Tool\Sitemap` writes a [sitemap](https://www.sitemaps.org/protocol.html): the list of pages that search engines should know about. Paths are made absolute URLs, and dates are written in the format sitemaps use.

## How to use

The skeleton serves `/sitemap.xml` with `Site\Controller\Sitemap`: the pages listed in `site/config/sitemap.php`, then every published news post.

```php title="htdocs/_fegg/site/src/Controller/Sitemap.php"
<?php
namespace Site\Controller;

defined('FEGG_CORE_DIR') || exit;

use Fegg\Model;
use Fegg\Tool\Sitemap as SitemapXml;

class Sitemap extends Controller implements \Fegg\Http\RouteOnly
{
    public function index()
    {
        $sitemap = new SitemapXml();
        foreach ($this->loadConfig('sitemap') as $path) {
            $sitemap->add($path);
        }

        // Published posts only: new Model(false, ...) leaves out the hidden ones
        $news = new Model(false, array(
            'table'   => 'cms_news',
            'id'      => 'news_id',
            'visible' => 'visible',
        ));
        foreach ($news->select('news_id, modified')->order('post_date', 'desc')->all() as $row) {
            $sitemap->add('/news/post_' . $row['news_id'] . '.html', $row['modified']);
        }

        $sitemap->display();
    }
}
```

```php title="htdocs/_fegg/site/config/sitemap.php"
$sitemap = array(
    '/',
    '/news/',
    '/contact/',
);
```

```php title="htdocs/_fegg/site/config/route.php"
$route['sitemap.xml'] = 'sitemap/index';
```

The controller is [route-only](../../basic/url.mdx#route-only-controllers): it answers `/sitemap.xml` and nothing else.

For a new project:

- Add the project's pages to `site/config/sitemap.php`, and add the posts of other CMS sections in the controller as for the news.
- Leave out pages that are not for search results: form steps, thank-you pages, the CMS.
- Give no date to the pages of `sitemap.php`: the dates of their files change on every deploy. Give the posts their `modified` date.

Tell search engines where the sitemap is with a line in `htdocs/robots.txt` (an absolute URL):

```text title="htdocs/robots.txt"
Sitemap: https://www.example.com/sitemap.xml
```

```xml title="Output"
<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
<urlset xmlns="http://www.sitemaps.org/schemas/sitemap/0.9">
  <url>
    <loc>https://www.example.com/</loc>
  </url>
  <url>
    <loc>https://www.example.com/news/post_12.html</loc>
    <lastmod>2026-11-15T10:00:00+09:00</lastmod>
  </url>
</urlset>
```

Only `<loc>` and `<lastmod>` are written: search engines ignore `<changefreq>` and `<priority>`. A sitemap lists at most 50,000 URLs.

## Absolute URLs

Paths are made absolute with `feggAbsoluteUrl()`, a function you can also call yourself (it is used by [JSON-LD](../json-ld/index.mdx) and [Feed](../feed/index.mdx) too):

```php
feggAbsoluteUrl('/news/');                       // https://www.example.com/news/
feggAbsoluteUrl('https://other.example.com/');   // returned as it is
feggAbsoluteUrl('/x/', 'https://example.org');   // https://example.org/x/
```

```php
string feggAbsoluteUrl( string $path [, string $baseUrl = '' ] )
```

- `$path` is a path of the site **without the base path**: `FEGG_CURRENT_URL` (the scheme, the host and the base path, like `{{ base_url }}`) is put before it. When the site is installed in `/sub/`, `'/news/'` becomes `https://example.com/sub/news/`.
- `http://` and `https://` URLs are returned as they are; `//host/...` gets the scheme of the request.
- `$baseUrl` replaces `FEGG_CURRENT_URL`, for example in a command, where there is no request.

The URLs use the host of the request: on a test server, the sitemap lists the test server's URLs.

A URL that already contains the base path, such as the result of `getImageUrl()`, only needs the domain: `FEGG_CURRENT_DOMAIN . getImageUrl($id)`.

## Errors

- A URL that is not `http` or `https`, or contains spaces or control characters, throws `\InvalidArgumentException`. So does a date that cannot be read.
- A 50,001st URL throws `\LengthException`.

## Methods

<CategoryDocCardList label="Methods" />


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/sitemap/methods/construct ---


# Sitemap::__construct

> Create an empty sitemap

## Description

```php
public Sitemap::__construct( [ string $baseUrl = '' ] )
```

`$baseUrl` is the root URL that paths are added to. `''` uses the URL of the current request (`FEGG_CURRENT_URL`: the scheme, the host and the base path).

## Arguments

| Name     | Data type | Default | Remarks |
| -------- | --------- | ------- | ------- |
| $baseUrl | string    | `''`    | Root URL of the site, with the base path. `''`: the current request |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Tool\Sitemap;

$sitemap = new Sitemap();                              // URLs of the current host
$sitemap = new Sitemap('https://www.example.com');     // always the production host
```

Pass the root URL when the sitemap is written outside a request (a command that saves it as a file).


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/sitemap/methods/add ---


# Sitemap::add

> Add a page

## Description

```php
public Sitemap Sitemap::add( string $loc [, string|int|\DateTimeInterface|null $lastmod = null ] )
```

Adds a page and returns the sitemap, so calls can be chained. A path is made absolute (see [Absolute URLs](../index.mdx#absolute-urls)). A URL added twice is listed once, with the date of the last call.

`$lastmod` is written as a W3C date with the time zone (`2026-11-15T10:00:00+09:00`). `null`, `''` and `'0000-00-00 00:00:00'` write no date.

Throws `\InvalidArgumentException` for a URL that is not `http` or `https` or a date that cannot be read, and `\LengthException` for more than 50,000 URLs.

## Arguments

| Name     | Data type | Default | Remarks |
| -------- | --------- | ------- | ------- |
| $loc     | string    |         | Path of the site without the base path (`'/news/'`), or an `http(s)` URL |
| $lastmod | string\|int\|DateTimeInterface\|null | `null` | When the page last changed: `'Y-m-d H:i:s'` from the database, a Unix time or a date object |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Tool\Sitemap;

$sitemap = new Sitemap();
$sitemap->add('/')
        ->add('/about/')
        ->add('/news/post_12.html', '2026-11-15 10:00:00');
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/sitemap/methods/count ---


# Sitemap::count

> Number of URLs added

## Description

```php
public int Sitemap::count()
```

Returns the number of URLs in the sitemap (a URL added twice counts once).

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Tool\Sitemap;

$sitemap = new Sitemap();
$sitemap->add('/')->add('/news/')->add('/');

$sitemap->count();   // 2
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/sitemap/methods/toxml ---


# Sitemap::toXml

> Return the sitemap XML

## Description

```php
public string Sitemap::toXml()
```

Returns the sitemap as XML text, in the order the URLs were added. [display()](./display.mdx) sends the same text.

## Example

```php
use Fegg\Tool\Sitemap;

$sitemap = new Sitemap('https://www.example.com');
$sitemap->add('/')->add('/news/post_12.html', '2026-11-15 10:00:00');

file_put_contents(FEGG_HTML_DIR . '/sitemap.xml', $sitemap->toXml());
```

```xml title="Output"
<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
<urlset xmlns="http://www.sitemaps.org/schemas/sitemap/0.9">
  <url>
    <loc>https://www.example.com/</loc>
  </url>
  <url>
    <loc>https://www.example.com/news/post_12.html</loc>
    <lastmod>2026-11-15T10:00:00+09:00</lastmod>
  </url>
</urlset>
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/sitemap/methods/display ---


# Sitemap::display

> Send the sitemap and end the response

## Description

```php
public void Sitemap::display()
```

Sends the sitemap with `Content-Type: application/xml; charset=UTF-8` and ends the response (it throws `Fegg\Http\Halt`, like `displayPage()`). Code after it does not run.

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Tool\Sitemap as SitemapXml;

public function index()
{
    $sitemap = new SitemapXml();
    foreach ($this->loadConfig('sitemap') as $path) {
        $sitemap->add($path);
    }
    $sitemap->display();
}
```

Inside a controller named `Sitemap`, import the library under another name (`as SitemapXml`), as the skeleton does.


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/trim ---


import { CategoryDocCardList } from '@site/src/components/CategoryDocCardList';

# Introduction

> Resizing and cropping images

`Fegg\Tool\Trim` resizes and crops JPEG, GIF and PNG images with the GD extension. JPEG images are rotated according to their Exif orientation.

## How to use

Import `Fegg\Tool\Trim` and create it with `new`. Set the source image and the output path with [Trim::setImage](./methods/setimage.mdx), resize it with one of the `resize*` methods, then save it with [Trim::create](./methods/create.mdx) or send it without saving with [Trim::output](./methods/output.mdx).

```php title="Controller"
<?php
namespace Site\Controller;

defined('FEGG_CORE_DIR') || exit;

use Fegg\Tool\Trim;

class Thumbnail extends Controller
{
    public function index()
    {
        $trim = new Trim();
        try {
            $trim->setImage('path/to/image.jpg', 'path/to/thumbnail/300_300.jpg');
        } catch (\RuntimeException $e) {
            http_response_code(404);
            $this->halt('Not Found');
        }

        $trim->resizeTrim(300, 300, 'm');
        if ($trim->create()) {
            // Output the saved image to the browser
            $trim->view();
        }
        $this->halt();
    }
}
```

## Images of the CMS in other sizes

The images uploaded in the CMS are shown in other sizes through URLs such as `/_fegg/site/up_dir/image/12/i-300_200.jpg`, built by the `getImageUrl` modifier (`{{ $page.data.image_id|getImageUrl:300:200 }}`).

```
/_fegg/site/up_dir/image/{id}/{type}-{width}_{height}.{jpg|png|gif}
/_fegg/site/up_dir/image/{id}/{type}-{width}.{jpg|png|gif}
/_fegg/site/up_dir/image/{id}/{type}.{jpg|png|gif}
```

`Fegg\Controller\Up_dir::image()` makes a missing size with this class and saves it at that path; later requests get the saved file.

| type | Method | Result |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `i` | [Trim::resize](./methods/resize.mdx) | Within the size, keeping the ratio |
| `c` | [Trim::resizeFill](./methods/resizefill.mdx) | Exactly the size, the empty area white or transparent |
| `f` | [Trim::resizeForce](./methods/resizeforce.mdx) | Stretched to the size |
| `lt` `t` `rt` `l` `m` `r` `lb` `b` `rb` | [Trim::resizeTrim](./methods/resizetrim.mdx) | Fills the size, keeping that part of the image |

A missing width or height is the original one. A name that is not one of these (`x-10.jpg`, `I-10.JPG`), an unknown image ID or a file that is not an image is a 404.

Two settings of `htdocs/_fegg/site/config/upload_dir.php` keep anyone from filling the disk or the memory with sizes:

| Key | Default | Remarks |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `image_max` | 2000 | Largest width or height that can be asked for. A larger one is a 404 |
| `image_cache_max` | 50 | How many sizes of one image are saved. Past that, new sizes are made for each request ([Trim::output](./methods/output.mdx)) and not saved |

```php title="htdocs/_fegg/site/config/upload_dir.php"
$upload_dir['image_max']       = 2000;
$upload_dir['image_cache_max'] = 50;
```

## Methods

<CategoryDocCardList label="Methods" />


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/trim/methods/setimage ---


# Trim::setImage

> Set target image and output file path

## Description

```php
public void Trim::setImage( string $org_path, string $cnv_path )
```

Loads the source image. The output format is decided by the extension of `$cnv_path`: `jpg` (or `jpeg`), `png` or `gif`. A JPEG photo is turned the way its Exif orientation says.

Throws a `\RuntimeException` when:

- `$org_path` is not a file;
- the extension of `$cnv_path` is not `jpg`, `jpeg`, `png` or `gif`;
- `$org_path` is not a JPEG, PNG or GIF image, or cannot be read.

## Arguments

| Name      | Data type | Default | Remarks                                                 |
| --------- | --------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------------------- |
| $org_path | string    |         | Source image path                                       |
| $cnv_path | string    |         | Output image path (`.jpg`, `.jpeg`, `.png` or `.gif`)   |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Tool\Trim;

$trim = new Trim();
try {
    $trim->setImage('path/to/image.jpg', 'path/to/converted.jpg');
} catch (\RuntimeException $e) {
    // missing file, not an image, or another output type
}
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/trim/methods/getsize ---


# Trim::getSize

> Get the width and height of the loaded image

## Description

```php
public array Trim::getSize()
```

Returns `array(width, height)` of the image loaded by [Trim::setImage](./setimage.mdx), after its Exif orientation (a photo taken upright gives the upright size).

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Tool\Trim;

$trim = new Trim();
$trim->setImage('path/to/image.jpg', 'path/to/converted.jpg');

list($width, $height) = $trim->getSize();
if ($width > $height) {
    $trim->resize(600, 0);
} else {
    $trim->resize(0, 600);
}
$trim->create();
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/trim/methods/resize ---


# Trim::resize

> Resize the image to fit in the specified size with keeping ratio

## Description

```php
public void Trim::resize( int $w, int $h )
```

The image is scaled down to fit in `$w` x `$h`, keeping its aspect ratio. The output may be smaller than `$w` x `$h` in one direction. Images smaller than the specified size are not enlarged.

## Arguments

| Name | Data type | Default | Remarks                                        |
| ---- | --------- | ------- | ---------------------------------------------- |
| $w   | int       |         | Maximum width / `0`: original width            |
| $h   | int       |         | Maximum height / `0`: original height          |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Tool\Trim;

$trim = new Trim();
$trim->setImage('path/to/image.jpg', 'path/to/converted.jpg');

// Width 300px at most, any height
$trim->resize(300, 0);
$trim->create();
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/trim/methods/resizefill ---


# Trim::resizeFill

> Resize the image with keeping ratio and filling the empty area (Like `object-fit: contain`)

## Description

```php
public void Trim::resizeFill( int $w, int $h )
```

The output is exactly `$w` x `$h`. The image is scaled down to fit in it, keeping its aspect ratio, and placed in the center. The empty area is transparent for PNG output (and for GIF output from a PNG or GIF source), white otherwise. Images smaller than the specified size are not enlarged.

## Arguments

| Name | Data type | Default | Remarks                                 |
| ---- | --------- | ------- | --------------------------------------- |
| $w   | int       |         | Output width / `0`: original width      |
| $h   | int       |         | Output height / `0`: original height    |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Tool\Trim;

$trim = new Trim();
$trim->setImage('path/to/image.png', 'path/to/converted.png');

$trim->resizeFill(300, 300);
$trim->create();
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/trim/methods/resizeforce ---


# Trim::resizeForce

> Resize the image to the specified size ignoring ratio

## Description

```php
public void Trim::resizeForce( int $w, int $h )
```

The image is stretched to exactly `$w` x `$h` (like `object-fit: fill`).

## Arguments

| Name | Data type | Default | Remarks                                 |
| ---- | --------- | ------- | --------------------------------------- |
| $w   | int       |         | Output width / `0`: original width      |
| $h   | int       |         | Output height / `0`: original height    |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Tool\Trim;

$trim = new Trim();
$trim->setImage('path/to/image.jpg', 'path/to/converted.jpg');

$trim->resizeForce(300, 300);
$trim->create();
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/trim/methods/resizetrim ---


# Trim::resizeTrim

> Resize the image with keeping ratio and trimming the overflow (Like `object-fit: cover`)

## Description

```php
public void Trim::resizeTrim( int $w, int $h, string $cp )
```

The output is exactly `$w` x `$h`. The image is scaled to cover the whole area, keeping its aspect ratio, and the overflow is cut off. `$cp` decides which part of the image is kept.

## Arguments

| Name | Data type | Default | Remarks                                 |
| ---- | --------- | ------- | --------------------------------------- |
| $w   | int       |         | Output width / `0`: original width      |
| $h   | int       |         | Output height / `0`: original height    |
| $cp  | string    |         | Crop position (see below)               |

### Crop position

The positions are listed in `Trim::POSITIONS`.

| $cp  | Kept part       |
| ---- | --------------- |
| `lt` | Top left        |
| `t`  | Top center      |
| `rt` | Top right       |
| `l`  | Middle left     |
| `m`  | Center          |
| `r`  | Middle right    |
| `lb` | Bottom left     |
| `b`  | Bottom center   |
| `rb` | Bottom right    |

Any other value is `lt`.

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Tool\Trim;

$trim = new Trim();
$trim->setImage('path/to/image.jpg', 'path/to/converted.jpg');

$trim->resizeTrim(300, 300, 'm');
$trim->create();
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/trim/methods/create ---


# Trim::create

> Save the image to the output path

## Description

```php
public bool Trim::create()
```

Saves the resized image to the output path given to [Trim::setImage](./setimage.mdx). Missing directories are created. JPEG images are saved with quality 90.

The image is written next to the output path first and then renamed, so a request at the same time never reads half a file. Returns `false` when it could not be saved.

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Tool\Trim;

$trim = new Trim();
$trim->setImage('path/to/image.jpg', 'path/to/converted.jpg');

$trim->resizeTrim(300, 300, 'm');
if (!$trim->create()) {
    // could not be saved
}
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/trim/methods/output ---


# Trim::output

> Output the image to the browser without saving it

## Description

```php
public void Trim::output()
```

Sends the `Content-Type` header of the output type and the resized image, without saving it. Use [Trim::create](./create.mdx) and [Trim::view](./view.mdx) to save it as well.

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Tool\Trim;

$trim = new Trim();
$trim->setImage('path/to/image.jpg', 'path/to/preview.jpg');

$trim->resize(600, 0);
$trim->output();
$this->halt();
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/trim/methods/view ---


# Trim::view

> Output the saved image to the browser

## Description

```php
public void Trim::view()
```

Sends the `Content-Type` header and outputs the file at the output path. Call [Trim::create](./create.mdx) first: nothing is output when the file does not exist. To send the image without saving it, use [Trim::output](./output.mdx).

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Tool\Trim;

$trim = new Trim();
$trim->setImage('path/to/image.jpg', 'path/to/converted.jpg');

$trim->resizeTrim(300, 300, 'm');
$trim->create();
$trim->view();
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/upload ---


import { CategoryDocCardList } from '@site/src/components/CategoryDocCardList';

# Introduction

> Uploads and file checks

## How to use

Import `Fegg\Tool\Upload` and create it with `new`. Set the upload directory, then call [Upload::upload](./methods/upload.mdx) with the `name` of the `input[type="file"]`.

```php title="Controller"
<?php
namespace Site\Controller;

defined('FEGG_CORE_DIR') || exit;

use Fegg\Tool\Upload;

class Entry extends Controller
{
    public function post()
    {
        $this->loadConfig('upload_dir');

        $upload = new Upload();
        $upload->setUploadDir($this->config['upload_dir']['path'] . '/entry');
        $upload->setMaxFileSize(5);
        $upload->setFileType(array('jpg', 'jpeg', 'png', 'pdf'));

        if ($upload->upload('file', uniqid('f_'), 'Attachment')) {
            $data = $upload->getUploadedData('file');
            // $data['name'], $data['org_name'], $data['type'], $data['size'] ...
        } else {
            $errors = $upload->getErrorMessage('file');
        }
    }
}
```

`$this->loadConfig('upload_dir')` reads `site/config/upload_dir.php` (`path` `htdocs/_fegg/site/up_dir` and `url` `/_fegg/site/up_dir`) into `$this->config['upload_dir']`. The CMS screens (`Fegg\Controller\Cms\Image`, `Media`, `Multi`) use this class in the same way.

Error messages are defined in `file_error_message.php`, see [Configuration](./configuration.mdx).

## What is checked

[Upload::upload](./methods/upload.mdx) checks, in order:

1. Upload errors and the size (`@empty`, `@size`, `@failed`).
2. The extension must be allowed by [setFileType](./methods/setfiletype.mdx). Script extensions (`php`, `phtml`, `phar`, `cgi` ...) are refused even when listed (`@type`).
3. The content must match the extension (`@content`): images (`jpg`, `jpeg`, `png`, `gif`, `webp`) must be readable by `getimagesize()`, and for the extensions of [Upload::MIME_TYPES](#mime-types) the MIME type reported by `finfo` must be one of the types listed for the extension. A PHP file renamed to `.jpg`, or a text file named `.pdf`, is refused.

When the file is saved:

- Files are saved under the name you give (`uniqid('f_')`, ...), never under the visitor's file name. Keep the original name (`org_name`) in the database only.
- Directories are created with mode `0755` and files saved with `0644`.

## MIME types

`Upload::MIME_TYPES` is the one table of types by extension (`jpg`, `png`, `gif`, `webp`, `pdf`, the Office formats such as `docx`, `xlsm`, `pptx`, and `zip`, `txt`, `csv`). For each extension:

- every type listed is accepted from `finfo` when the file is uploaded;
- the first type is the one the stored file is sent with ([Upload::mimeType](./methods/mimetype.mdx)).

```php
Upload::MIME_TYPES['docx'];
// array('application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.document', 'application/zip', 'application/octet-stream')
```

An extension that is not in the table is checked by its extension only, and sent with the type `finfo` reads from it.

## Protecting the upload directory

The upload directory `htdocs/_fegg/site/up_dir/` is inside `_fegg/`, which is denied; the root `.htaccess` lets `/_fegg/site/up_dir/` through and sends a file that is not there (an image size not made yet, `/file/<id>/`) to PHP. When the upload directory is below the document root, [setUploadDir](./methods/setuploaddir.mdx) writes an `.htaccess` into it (`Upload::HTACCESS`, an existing file is left alone) that serves only the file types that can be uploaded and runs no script, so an uploaded file can never run as code. The CMS dashboard also writes it when it opens, and the skeleton ships the file.

`fegg-cli doctor` and the CMS dashboard report a missing `site/up_dir/.htaccess` or a missing root rule (see [fegg-cli](../../first/cli.mdx#doctor)). Remember it when you upload over FTP: many FTP clients skip dotfiles. On nginx, `.htaccess` is ignored: use the `location ^~ /_fegg/site/up_dir/` block of [Directory structure](../../first/cont.mdx#protecting-_fegg).

## Sending stored files

The CMS sends stored files (`/_fegg/site/up_dir/file/<id>/`, the media and image screens) with `Fegg\Http\Download::sendHeaders()`. It sends `X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff`, a valid `Cache-Control`, and an RFC 6266 `Content-Disposition` with the original name escaped (an ASCII fallback plus the UTF-8 name). HTML, SVG, XML and JavaScript files are always sent as attachments, never shown inline. Use it when your own controller sends a stored file:

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Http\Download;

Download::sendHeaders(array(
    'type'     => $file['type'],
    'size'     => filesize($path),
    'org_name' => $file['org_name'],
), false);              // false: browser cache only (private); true: public
$this->halt(file_get_contents($path));
```

# Configuration

The error messages of [Upload](./index.mdx) are defined in `htdocs/_fegg/core/config/file_error_message.php`.

To change them, copy the file to `site/config/` (see [Overriding the core](../../first/cont.mdx#overriding-the-core)). The whole file is replaced, so keep every key.

```php title="htdocs/_fegg/core/config/file_error_message.php"
<?php
defined('FEGG_CORE_DIR') || exit;

/**
 * Error messages for the Tool/File class.
 * @see Tool/File.php
 */
// @file is too big.
$file_error_message['@size']   = '<strong>%s</strong> のサイズが大きすぎます';
// @file is not selected specified type.
$file_error_message['@type']   = '<strong>%s</strong> が指定の型式で選択されていません';
// @file failed to upload.
$file_error_message['@failed'] = '<strong>%s</strong> のアップロードに失敗しました';
// @file's content does not match its extension.
$file_error_message['@content'] = '<strong>%s</strong> の内容がファイル形式と一致しません';
// @file is not selected.
$file_error_message['@empty']  = '<strong>%s</strong> が選択されていません';
```

`%s` is replaced with the `$label` passed to [Upload::upload](./methods/upload.mdx).

| Code      | When                                                                  |
| --------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `@empty`  | No file was selected (only when the file is required)                 |
| `@size`   | The file is larger than the maximum size, or than the php.ini / form limit |
| `@type`   | The extension is not one of the allowed file types, or is a script extension |
| `@content` | The content does not match the extension (see [What is checked](./index.mdx#what-is-checked)) |
| `@failed` | Any other upload error, or the file could not be moved to the upload directory |

## Other languages

When a language code is passed to the constructor (`new Upload('en')`), the messages are read from `config/en/file_error_message.php` (searched in `site/config/` first, then `core/config/`).


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/upload/methods/construct ---


# Upload::\_\_construct

> Create the uploader and load the error messages

## Description

```php
public Upload::__construct( [ string $languageCode = null ] )
```

Loads the error messages from `file_error_message.php` (see [Configuration](../configuration.mdx)). The default settings are: no upload directory, a maximum file size of 2 MB, and the file types `jpg`, `jpeg`, `png` and `gif`.

## Arguments

| Name          | Data type | Default | Remarks                                                          |
| ------------- | --------- | ------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------- |
| $languageCode | string    | null    | Language code / if set, `config/{languageCode}/file_error_message.php` is used |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Tool\Upload;

$upload = new Upload();

// English error messages from config/en/file_error_message.php
$upload = new Upload('en');
```

# Upload::setUploadDir

> Set the upload directory

## Description

```php
public boolean Upload::setUploadDir( string $dir )
```

The directory is created (recursively, mode `0755`) when it does not exist. Returns `true` when the directory is writable, otherwise `false` and the directory is not set.

When the directory is below the document root and has no `.htaccess`, `Upload::HTACCESS` is written there: it serves only the file types that can be uploaded and runs no script (see [Introduction](../index.mdx#protecting-the-upload-directory)). An existing `.htaccess` is left alone.

## Arguments

| Name | Data type | Default | Remarks          |
| ---- | --------- | ------- | ---------------- |
| $dir | string    |         | Upload directory |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Tool\Upload;

$upload = new Upload();
$upload->setUploadDir($this->config['upload_dir']['path']);
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/upload/methods/getuploaddir ---

```php
public string Upload::getUploadDir()
```

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Tool\Upload;

```


# Upload::setFileType

> Set the allowed file types (extensions)

## Description

```php
public boolean Upload::setFileType( array $type )
```

Replaces the list of allowed extensions. The extensions are compared in lower case. Returns `false` when `$type` is not an array. The default is `array('jpg', 'jpeg', 'png', 'gif')`.

Extensions a web server may run as code (`php`, `php5`, `phtml`, `phar`, `pht`, `phps`, `cgi`, `pl`, `py`, `rb`, `sh`, `asp`, `aspx`, `jsp`, `shtml`, `htaccess`, `htpasswd`) are **always refused**, even when listed here. The file's content must also match its extension (see [Upload::upload](./upload.mdx)).

## Arguments

| Name  | Data type | Default | Remarks                                  |
| ----- | --------- | ------- | ---------------------------------------- |
| $type | array     |         | Allowed extensions, without the leading dot |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Tool\Upload;

$upload = new Upload();
$upload->setFileType(array('pdf', 'docx', 'xlsx'));
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/upload/methods/getfiletype ---


# Upload::getFileType

> Get the allowed file types (extensions)

## Description

```php
public array Upload::getFileType()
```

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Tool\Upload;

$upload = new Upload();
var_dump($upload->getFileType()); // -> array('jpg', 'jpeg', 'png', 'gif')
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/upload/methods/setuploadinfo ---


# Upload::setUploadInfo

> Set the upload options at once

## Description

```php
public boolean Upload::setUploadInfo( array $data )
```

Calls [Upload::setUploadDir](./setuploaddir.mdx), [Upload::setMaxFileSize](./setmaxfilesize.mdx) and [Upload::setFileType](./setfiletype.mdx) for the keys present in `$data`. Returns `false` when one of them fails.

## Arguments

| Name  | Data type | Default | Remarks                    |
| ----- | --------- | ------- | -------------------------- |
| $data | array     |         | Options (see below)        |

| Key         | Data type | Remarks                     |
| ----------- | --------- | --------------------------- |
| upDir       | string    | Upload directory            |
| maxFileSize | numeric   | Maximum file size (MB)      |
| fileType    | array     | Allowed extensions          |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Tool\Upload;

$upload = new Upload();
$upload->setUploadInfo(array(
    'upDir'       => $this->config['upload_dir']['path'] . '/entry',
    'maxFileSize' => 5,
    'fileType'    => array('jpg', 'png', 'pdf'),
));
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/upload/methods/upload ---


# Upload::upload

> Validate uploaded file and move to upload directory

## Description

```php
public boolean Upload::upload( string $name, string $up_name, [ string $label = 'ファイル', boolean $required = true ] )
```

Checks the file posted as `$_FILES[$name]` and moves it to `{upload directory}/{$up_name}.{extension}` with mode `0644`. The extension is the lower-cased extension of the original file name.

The size, the extension and the content are checked first (see [What is checked](../index.mdx#what-is-checked)).

Returns `true` on success. On failure it returns `false` and the error messages are available with [Upload::getErrorMessage](./geterrormessage.mdx).

- No file selected, or a field that the form does not have (no `$_FILES[$name]`): `@empty`, or `true` without saving anything when `$required` is `false`.
- Several files in one field (`name="file[]"`) are not handled by this method: the result is `@failed`. Give each file its own field.

Neither case writes a PHP warning.

Set the upload directory with [Upload::setUploadDir](./setuploaddir.mdx) before calling this method.

## Arguments

| Name      | Data type | Default    | Remarks                                                   |
| --------- | --------- | ---------- | --------------------------------------------------------- |
| $name     | string    |            | `name` attribute of the `input[type="file"]`              |
| $up_name  | string    |            | File name to save, without extension                      |
| $label    | string    | 'ファイル' | Item label used in the error messages                     |
| $required | boolean   | true       | `false`: no error when no file is selected                |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Tool\Upload;

$upload = new Upload();
$upload->setUploadDir($this->config['upload_dir']['path']);

if ($upload->upload('image', uniqid('i_'), 'Image')) {
    $data = $upload->getUploadedData('image');
} else {
    $errors = $upload->getErrorMessage('image');
}

// Optional file
$upload->upload('attachment', uniqid('a_'), 'Attachment', false);
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/upload/methods/getuploadeddata ---


# Upload::getUploadedData

> Get the information of the uploaded file

## Description

```php
public mixed Upload::getUploadedData( string $name )
```

Returns an array describing the file saved by [Upload::upload](./upload.mdx), or `null` when no file was saved for `$name`.

| Key      | Remarks                                                      |
| -------- | ------------------------------------------------------------ |
| name     | Saved file name (`$up_name` + extension)                     |
| org_name | Original file name                                           |
| type     | MIME type: from `getimagesize()` for images, otherwise [Upload::mimeType](./mimetype.mdx) |
| size     | File size (bytes)                                            |
| up_time  | Upload time (Unix timestamp)                                 |
| width    | Image width (images only)                                    |
| height   | Image height (images only)                                   |
| attr     | `width="..." height="..."` string (images only)              |

## Arguments

| Name  | Data type | Default | Remarks                                      |
| ----- | --------- | ------- | -------------------------------------------- |
| $name | string    |         | `name` attribute of the `input[type="file"]` |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Tool\Upload;

$upload = new Upload();
$upload->setUploadDir($this->config['upload_dir']['path']);

if ($upload->upload('image', uniqid('i_'))) {
    $data = $upload->getUploadedData('image');
    // $data['name'] -> "i_65f0c3a1b2c4d.jpg"
}
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/upload/methods/iserror ---


# Upload::isError

> Check whether any upload error occurred

## Description

```php
public boolean Upload::isError()
```

Returns `true` once an error has occurred in any [Upload::upload](./upload.mdx) call on this object. Before that it returns `null` (falsy).

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Tool\Upload;

$upload = new Upload();
$upload->setUploadDir($this->config['upload_dir']['path']);

$upload->upload('image1', uniqid('i_'), 'Image 1');
$upload->upload('image2', uniqid('i_'), 'Image 2');

if ($upload->isError()) {
    // At least one of the files failed
}
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/upload/methods/geterrormessage ---


# Upload::getErrorMessage

> Get the error messages of a file

## Description

```php
public mixed Upload::getErrorMessage( string $name )
```

Returns the error messages of the last [Upload::upload](./upload.mdx) call for `$name` as an array, or `null` when there is no error. The messages are defined in `file_error_message.php` (see [Configuration](../configuration.mdx)).

## Arguments

| Name  | Data type | Default | Remarks                                      |
| ----- | --------- | ------- | -------------------------------------------- |
| $name | string    |         | `name` attribute of the `input[type="file"]` |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Tool\Upload;

$upload = new Upload();
$upload->setUploadDir($this->config['upload_dir']['path']);

if (!$upload->upload('image', uniqid('i_'), 'Image')) {
    var_dump($upload->getErrorMessage('image'));
    // -> array('<strong>Image</strong> のサイズが大きすぎます')
}
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/upload/methods/mimetype ---


# Upload::mimeType

> Get the MIME type a stored file is sent with

## Description

```php
public static string Upload::mimeType( string $path, string $extension )
```

Returns the first type of `Upload::MIME_TYPES` for `$extension` (case does not matter). For an extension that is not in the table, returns the type `finfo` reads from the file `$path`, or `application/octet-stream` when it cannot be read.

[Upload::upload](./upload.mdx) uses it for the `type` of a file that is not an image ([Upload::getUploadedData](./getuploadeddata.mdx)).

## Arguments

| Name       | Data type | Default | Remarks                    |
| ---------- | --------- | ------- | -------------------------- |
| $path      | string    |         | Path of the stored file    |
| $extension | string    |         | Extension of the file      |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Tool\Upload;

echo Upload::mimeType($path, 'pdf');   // -> "application/pdf"
echo Upload::mimeType($path, 'XLSX');  // -> "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet"
echo Upload::mimeType($path, 'svg');   // -> what finfo reads, e.g. "image/svg+xml"
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/validation ---


import { CategoryDocCardList } from '@site/src/components/CategoryDocCardList';

# Introduction

> Form validation

## How to use

Import `Fegg\Tool\Validation` and create it with `new`. Call a check method for each field, then read the result with [Validation::getErrorMessage](./methods/geterrormessage.mdx).

```php title="Controller"
<?php
namespace Site\Controller;

defined('FEGG_CORE_DIR') || exit;

use Fegg\Tool\Validation;

class Contact extends Controller
{
    public function confirm()
    {
        $validation = new Validation();

        $id   = 'name';
        $name = 'お名前';
        $validation->required($id, $this->in($id), array('@required', $name));
        $validation->maxlength($id, $this->in($id), 50, array('@maxlength', $name, 50));

        $id   = 'mail';
        $name = 'メールアドレス';
        $validation->required($id, $this->in($id), array('@required', $name));
        $validation->email($id, $this->in($id), array('@email', $name));

        $errorMessage = $validation->getErrorMessage();
        if (!$errorMessage) {
            $this->displayPage('contact/confirm');
        } else {
            $this->page['error'] = $errorMessage;
            $this->displayPage('contact/input');
        }
    }
}
```

## How checks work

- Every check method takes the field name `$name` first. It is the key of the error message returned by [Validation::getErrorMessage](./methods/geterrormessage.mdx).
- Only the first error of each field is kept: once a field has an error, the next checks of the same field are skipped and return `false`. Write the checks in order of priority (e.g. `required` before `email`).
- Except [Validation::required](./methods/required.mdx) and [Validation::upload](./methods/upload.mdx), the checks do not check an empty value (they return `true`). Use `required` for required fields.
- `$code` decides the error message, see [Configuration](./configuration.mdx).

## Methods

<CategoryDocCardList label="Methods" />


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/validation/configuration ---


# Configuration

## Error messages

The error messages are defined in `htdocs/_fegg/core/config/validation_error_message.php`.

To change them, copy the file to `site/config/` (see [Overriding the core](../../first/cont.mdx#overriding-the-core)). The whole file is replaced, so keep every key you use.

```php title="htdocs/_fegg/core/config/validation_error_message.php"
<?php
defined('FEGG_CORE_DIR') || exit;

/**
 * Error messages for the Validation class.
 *
 * @see Validation.php
 */
// @item should be Alphanumeric.
$validation_error_message['@alphameric'] = '<strong>%s</strong> は半角英数で入力してください';
// @item is not Date format.
$validation_error_message['@date'] = '<strong>%s</strong> の日付では登録できません';
// @item is not Email format.
$validation_error_message['@email'] = '<strong>%s</strong> は登録できない形式です';
// @item should be half size character.
$validation_error_message['@hankaku'] = '<strong>%s</strong> は半角で入力してください';
// @item should be KATAKANA.
$validation_error_message['@katakana'] = '<strong>%s</strong> はカタカナで入力してください';
// @item must be at most N characters long.
$validation_error_message['@maxlength'] = '<strong>%s</strong> は <strong>%s</strong> 文字以内で入力してください';
// @item must be at least N characters long.
$validation_error_message['@minlength'] = '<strong>%s</strong> は <strong>%s</strong> 文字以上で入力してください';
// @item should be Numeric.
$validation_error_message['@numeric'] = '<strong>%s</strong> は 半角数字で入力してください';
// @item should be Numeric to Integers or Decimals.
$validation_error_message['@numeric_decimalpoint'] = '<strong>%s</strong> は 半角の整数もしくは小数で入力してください';
// @item should be half size character to Numeric and Minus sign.
$validation_error_message['@numeric_minus'] = '<strong>%s</strong> は 数字、マイナス符号とも半角で入力してください';
// @item is unable character type.
$validation_error_message['@password'] = '<strong>%s</strong> は登録できない形式です';
// @item should be mixing of alphanumeric.
$validation_error_message['@password_mixed_letters'] = '<strong>%s</strong> は半角英数の混合で入力してください';
// @item is required.
$validation_error_message['@required'] = '<strong>%s</strong> をご入力ください';
// @item is not Tel format.
$validation_error_message['@tel'] = '<strong>%s</strong> は登録できない形式です';
// @item is not Time format.
$validation_error_message['@time'] = '<strong>%s</strong> の時間では登録できません';
// @item is not Zipcode format.
$validation_error_message['@zipcode'] = '<strong>%s</strong> は登録できない形式です';
```

There are no codes for [url](./methods/url.mdx), [userid](./methods/userid.mdx), [length](./methods/length.mdx) and [maxbyte](./methods/maxbyte.mdx). Pass the message itself, or add your own codes to the copy in `site/config/`.

## The `$code` argument

The `$code` argument of the check methods, [Validation::setError](./methods/seterror.mdx) and [Validation::upload](./methods/upload.mdx) can be:

| `$code` | Error message |
| --- | --- |
| `'@required'` | The message of the code as it is |
| `array('@required', 'Name')` | The message of the first element, with each `%s` replaced by the following elements in order (`vsprintf()`). An element that is itself a code (e.g. `'@foo'`) is replaced by its message |
| `'Please enter your name'` | A string that is not a code is used as the message |

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Tool\Validation;

$validation = new Validation();

$validation->maxlength('name', $this->in('name'), 50, array('@maxlength', 'お名前', 50));
// -> '<strong>お名前</strong> は <strong>50</strong> 文字以内で入力してください'

$validation->required('agree', $this->in('agree'), '<strong>利用規約</strong> に同意してください');
// -> '<strong>利用規約</strong> に同意してください'
```

## Other languages

When a language code is passed to the constructor (`new Validation('en')`), the messages are read from `config/en/validation_error_message.php` (searched in `site/config/` first, then `core/config/`). The core has no language files: create the file in `site/config/en/`.


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/validation/methods/construct ---


# Validation::\_\_construct

> Create the validator and load the error messages

## Description

```php
public Validation::__construct( [ string $languageCode = '' ] )
```

Loads the error messages from `validation_error_message.php` (see [Configuration](../configuration.mdx)). The file in `site/config/` is used when it exists, otherwise the core's one.

## Arguments

| Name          | Data type | Default | Remarks                                                                                                    |
| ------------- | --------- | ------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| $languageCode | string    | ''      | Language code / if set, `config/{languageCode}/validation_error_message.php` is used (the file must exist) |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Tool\Validation;

$validation = new Validation();

// English error messages from config/en/validation_error_message.php
$validationEn = new Validation('en');
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/validation/methods/required ---


# Validation::required

> Check required

## Description

```php
public boolean Validation::required( string $name, mixed $value, [ mixed $code = '' ] )
```

A string is valid when it is not empty after trimming (`'0'` is valid). For an array, only the first element is checked: it must not be `''` or `null`.

## Arguments

| Name   | Data type | Default | Remarks                                                                                                      |
| ------ | --------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| $name  | string    |         | Field name (key of the error message)                                                                        |
| $value | mixed     |         | Value to check                                                                                               |
| $code  | mixed     | ''      | Error code, array of error code and parameters, or error message (see [Configuration](../configuration.mdx)) |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Tool\Validation;

$validation = new Validation();

$validation->required('name', 'John', array('@required', 'Name')); // -> true
$validation->required('mail', '', array('@required', 'Email'));    // -> false

var_dump($validation->isError()); // -> true
var_dump($validation->getErrorMessage());
// -> array('mail' => '<strong>Email</strong> をご入力ください')
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/validation/methods/alphameric ---


# Validation::alphameric

```php
public boolean Validation::alphameric( string $name, mixed $value, [ mixed $code = '' ] )
```

Valid when the value contains only `0-9`, `a-z` and `A-Z`.

## Arguments

| ------ | --------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| $name  | string    |         | Field name (key of the error message)                                                                        |
| $value | mixed     |         | Value to check                                                                                               |
| $code  | mixed     | ''      | Error code, array of error code and parameters, or error message (see [Configuration](../configuration.mdx)) |

## Example

--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/validation/methods/numeric ---


# Validation::numeric

> Check numeric or not

## Description

```php
public boolean Validation::numeric( string $name, mixed $value, [ mixed $code = '', boolean $decimalPointFlag = false, boolean $minusFlag = false ] )
```

Valid when the value contains only the digits `0-9`. `$decimalPointFlag` also allows a decimal part (`12.5`) and `$minusFlag` a leading `-`. Use the error codes `@numeric_decimalpoint` and `@numeric_minus` for the messages of those cases.

## Arguments

| Name              | Data type | Default | Remarks                                                                                                      |
| ----------------- | --------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| $name             | string    |         | Field name (key of the error message)                                                                        |
| $value            | mixed     |         | Value to check                                                                                               |
| $code             | mixed     | ''      | Error code, array of error code and parameters, or error message (see [Configuration](../configuration.mdx)) |
| $decimalPointFlag | boolean   | false   | `true`: allow a decimal part                                                                                 |
| $minusFlag        | boolean   | false   | `true`: allow a leading `-`                                                                                  |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Tool\Validation;

$validation = new Validation();

$validation->numeric('success', '123', array('@numeric', 'Quantity')); // -> true
$validation->numeric('failed', '12.5', array('@numeric', 'Quantity')); // -> false

var_dump($validation->getErrorMessage());
// -> array('failed' => '<strong>Quantity</strong> は 半角数字で入力してください')

// Decimals
$validation->numeric('price', '12.5', array('@numeric_decimalpoint', 'Price'), true); // -> true

// Negative numbers
$validation->numeric('offset', '-10', array('@numeric_minus', 'Offset'), false, true); // -> true
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/validation/methods/date ---


# Validation::date

> Check date format

## Description

```php
public boolean Validation::date( string $name, mixed $value, [ mixed $code = '' ] )
```

The separators `/` and `-` are removed, then the value must be digits only: `YYYY` (a year), `YYYYMM` (a year and a month from 01 to 12) or `YYYYMMDD` (an existing date, checked with `checkdate()`). So `2024`, `2024/04`, `2024-04-01` and `20240401` are valid; `2024/4/1` is not (8 digits are required for a day).

## Arguments

| Name   | Data type | Default | Remarks                                                                                                      |
| ------ | --------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| $name  | string    |         | Field name (key of the error message)                                                                        |
| $value | mixed     |         | Value to check                                                                                               |
| $code  | mixed     | ''      | Error code, array of error code and parameters, or error message (see [Configuration](../configuration.mdx)) |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Tool\Validation;

$validation = new Validation();

$validation->date('success', '2024-04-01', array('@date', 'Date')); // -> true
$validation->date('failed', '2024-02-30', array('@date', 'Date')); // -> false

var_dump($validation->getErrorMessage());
// -> array('failed' => '<strong>Date</strong> の日付では登録できません')
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/validation/methods/email ---

## Example

# Validation::tel

Valid when the value contains only digits, `-`, `(` and `)`, optionally starts with `+` (country code), and ends with a digit. For example `03-1234-5678`, `(03)1234-5678` and `+81-3-1234-5678`.

```php title="Controller"
$validation->tel('failed', '03 1234 5678', array('@tel', 'Tel')); // -> false

var_dump($validation->getErrorMessage());
// -> array('failed' => '<strong>Tel</strong> は登録できない形式です')
```

# Validation::time

> Check time format

## Description

```php
public boolean Validation::time( string $name, mixed $value, [ mixed $code = '' ] )
```

The separator `:` is removed, then the value must be 4 digits `HHMM` in 24-hour format (`00:00` to `23:59`). `9:30` is not valid: write `09:30`.

## Arguments

| Name   | Data type | Default | Remarks                                                                                                      |
| ------ | --------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| $name  | string    |         | Field name (key of the error message)                                                                        |
| $value | mixed     |         | Value to check                                                                                               |
| $code  | mixed     | ''      | Error code, array of error code and parameters, or error message (see [Configuration](../configuration.mdx)) |

## Example

```php title="Controller"
use Fegg\Tool\Validation;

$validation = new Validation();

$validation->time('success', '09:30', array('@time', 'Time')); // -> true
$validation->time('failed', '24:00', array('@time', 'Time')); // -> false

var_dump($validation->getErrorMessage());
// -> array('failed' => '<strong>Time</strong> の時間では登録できません')
```


--- https://lionheart-group.github.io/coding-style-guide/docs/fegg/next/library/validation/methods/url ---


# Validation::url

> Check URL format

## Description

```php
public boolean Validation::url( string $name, mixed $value, [ mixed $code = '' ] )
```

Valid when the value starts with `http://`, `https://` or `ftp://` and contains only the characters allowed in a URL.

## Arguments

| Name   | Data type | Default | Remarks                                                                                                      |
| ------ | --------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| $name  | string    |         | Field name (key of the error message)                                                                        |
| $value | mixed     |         | Value to check                                                                                               |
| $code  | mixed     | ''      | Error code, array of error code and parameters, or error message (see [Configuration](../configuration.mdx)) |

## Example

```php title="Controller"

$validation->url('success', 'https://example.com/', '<strong>URL</strong> の形式が正しくありません'); // -> true
$validation->url('failed', 'javascript:alert(1)', '<strong>URL</strong> の形式が正しくありません'); // -> false

// -> array('failed' => '<strong>URL</strong> の形式が正しくありません')
```
